  SELECTIVITY]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
     [Tower: L3 CORE]
     System transmits correctable axis to successors, not
     locked state. Fidelity necessary but insufficient.
     Failure: Fertile Lock.
     SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY = within-window axis integrity.
     TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY = what crosses transmission
     boundary.
     Primary proof: Feanor -> sons [distinction clearest case].
     Test: correctable axis transmitted?

CORE THEORY ADDITION FILTER:
  Add only if: [1] new confirmed structural property with
  multiple independent case confirmations, [2] promoted from
  extended theory [already vetted], or [3] resolves existing
  open limit or paradox.
  Reject: single-case findings, interesting observations without
  cross-domain confirmation, speculative extensions.

NESTED CYCLE MODEL [primary organizing structure -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Three cycles, each wrapping the one inside it. Replaces tower
as primary organizing structure. Tower vocabulary retained:
L1=C1, L2=C2, L3=C3 [nesting depth, not dependency sequence].
Failure = interference between cycles, not cascade down levels.

CENTER [point-truth -- unreachable, structural constant]
  The nested cycles organize around a center they cannot reach.
  Not a location inside the torus -- the axis threading through
  the whole structure. What all three cycles are oriented toward.
  Geometrically: the hole of the torus is not the center. The
  center is the axis the hole organizes around -- present as
  structural constant, inaccessible from inside any cycle.
  Relationship to True Coherence: same logic. True Coherence is
  a direction vector not a position. The center is what makes
  the direction coherent -- it gives the cycles their orientation
  without being reachable by any of them.
  Why unreachable: each cycle accesses truth through its function
  [C1 generates, C2 reads, C3 transmits]. None of these IS the
  truth. They approach it asymptotically. A system claiming to
  have reached the center has confused its cycle function with
  the axis it orbits.
  Constant: the center does not move when cycles degrade. False
  Coherence doesn't relocate the center -- it just loses
  orientation to it. The axis is still there; the cycles are
  running on a captured surface that points elsewhere.

TRUTH [operational definition -- v17 amended]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  A claim produced by examined axis, measured by declared
  instrument, revisable on evidence, producing behavioral
  adjustment when wrong.
  All four conditions required at the system's functional level.
  Absence of any one = truth claim not operational truth.

  LEVEL-DEPENDENT TRUTH [v17 amendment]:
    Truth is not blocked below L4. Each cycle level produces
    operational truth within its available resolution. L4
    INTEGRATION is the best version of behavioral adjustment --
    not the floor requirement.
      C1 truth: generative but unreadable to the system itself.
        Behavioral adjustment occurs through cycle correction,
        not examined revision. Pre-axial organisms operate here.
        Truth at this level = cycle corrects against the
        deviation. Narrow scope; genuine.
      C2 truth: readable and correctable. Behavioral adjustment
        fires when C2 surface is honest. Cannot transmit the
        revision process to successors -- corrections are local.
        This is where most examined individual truth operates.
      C3 truth: transmissible. Behavioral adjustment crosses
        generation boundaries via correctable method. Each
        successor can replicate the revision. L4 not yet
        present.
      L4 truth: self-correcting transmission. Behavioral
        adjustment reaches the correction methodology itself
        -- the system revises how it revises. Highest
        resolution available.
    Implication: a C2-functional system producing honest
    behavioral adjustment IS producing operational truth.
    Calling it inadequate because L4 INTEGRATION is absent is
    demanding the best version as the only valid version.
    The asymptote applies at every level. Any system is as
    close to the center as its highest functional cycle allows.

  FOUR CONDITIONS [level-dependent interpretation]:
    Examined axis [GENUINE AXIS]: the claim must come from
      axis that has been tested, not substituted. Applies at
      all levels; at C1 this is pre-verbal somatic axis
      examination. At L4 this includes meta-examination of the
      examination process itself.
    Declared instrument [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]: the
      instrument producing the claim must be named and its
      blind spots stated before the reading. Undeclared
      instrument = unverifiable claim. T3 applies to all
      readings; naming it is the requirement, not eliminating
      it.
    Revisable on evidence [CORRECTION CAPACITY]: the claim
      must specify what evidence would change it. Non-
      revisable claims are not truth claims -- they are
      identity statements. The inability to name falsification
      criteria is diagnostic of axis capture, not certainty.
      [See GA Verification Protocol -- same test.]
    Behavioral adjustment when wrong [CORRECTION CAPACITY at
      available level]: truth that produces no adjustment when
      falsified was not genuinely held. Adjustment is the
      proof of reception. At L4 this is INTEGRATION -- the
      correction methodology itself changes. At C2 this is
      object-level correction firing. Both are genuine
      adjustment; the scope differs.

  SELF-CRITICAL THOUGHT as structural requirement:
    Not an optional virtue. Self-critical capacity is what
    keeps the examined axis examined -- without ongoing
    self-critical pressure, axis drifts toward capture without
    the system noticing. The requirement is not "be humble"
    but "maintain the instrument that detects your own
    instrument's failure." Systems that suppress self-critical
    thought are not more confident -- they have eliminated the
    mechanism that would tell them when they're wrong.

  USEFUL REACTIONS as output requirement:
    Truth that produces no useful reaction is either not
    received [T9], not integrated at available level, or not
    actually about anything actionable. A truth claim should
    be evaluable by its downstream effects. Does knowing this
    change what the system does? If no: the claim may be
    accurate but is not operational truth for this system at
    this time. Operational truth is not just correct -- it is
    correct AND actionable AND adjusted-to at the level the
    system can manage.

  Relationship to center: operational truth is a cycle
    function oriented toward the center, not arrival at it.
    The best available truth-claim at any moment is the
    closest current orientation given available cycle depth.
    Higher cycle depth = higher resolution orientation; not
    a different center.

EVIL [operational definition -- v17 amended]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Surplus routed to minority at majority cost, with correction
  capacity absent or inverted.
  Three conditions required:
    Identifiable Boundary B bearing the cost [majority].
    Identifiable Boundary A capturing the benefit [minority].
    Correction capacity absent or inverted.

  CC ABSENT vs CC INVERTED [v17 amendment -- distinction now
    load-bearing]:
    CC ABSENT: no correction fires at all. Extraction continues
      because nothing stops it. No actor owns the correction
      gap -- the gap simply exists.
      Intervention requirement: build or enable correction
      capacity. Can be done without directly threatening the
      extraction architecture -- correction is absent, not
      defended.
      Degree: lower end of evil scale. Extraction without
      active defense is more correctable than extraction that
      defends itself.
    CC INVERTED: correction fires, aimed at extractor benefit.
      Correction exists and is functional -- its target is
      wrong. The mechanism that would stop extraction is
      instead protecting it.
      Intervention requirement: counter-capture before
      building. Restoring CC into an inverted system fires
      the new capacity toward the monopolist. Building
      correction capacity into a captured system accelerates
      extraction until the capture is broken first.
      Degree: higher severity. Inversion means the correction
      mechanism actively defends against the populations
      that would use it. Inversion is harder to detect than
      absence -- the surface shows correction activity;
      only the target reveals inversion.
    Both satisfy the three-condition definition. Degree and
    intervention path differ based on which condition holds.
    Degree scales with [1] magnitude of cost to Boundary B,
    [2] concentration of benefit to Boundary A, [3] how
    completely correction capacity is eliminated or inverted.
    [Implication: Diagnostic Collapse risk is highest under
    inverted CC. C2 restoration fires correction burst that
    is immediately captured and redirected. C1 substrate
    audit required before C2 intervention in inverted-CC
    systems, not only in absent-CC systems.]

  MEASUREMENT vs JUDGMENT [separation -- load-bearing]:
    Measurement: the framework reads structural pattern.
      Extraction occurring, direction of surplus, correction
      capacity status. Objective, structural, not requiring
      a judge. Framework work only.
    Judgment: a separate act by a person applying values to
      a specific situation. Evil in the moral sense requires
      this act. The framework cannot make it. A person must.
    Both required for coherent moral assessment. Measurement
      without judgment = structurally accurate, morally
      neutral. Judgment without measurement = arbitrary.

  WILLFUL EXTRACTION [evil by definition]:
    Actor knows extraction is occurring. Knows the cost to
      Boundary B. Continues.
    Knowledge plus continuation = evil without requiring
      additional argument. This is the clearest case -- no
      ambiguity about intent, no structural excuse.
    Framework confirms the structural reading.
    The judgment is the person's to make.

  DIRECTIONAL RULE: any extraction moves in the direction
    of evil regardless of scale, intent, or justification.
    Degree affects intervention priority and response type,
    not directional classification. Minor extraction with
    intact correction capacity is less urgent than inverted
    correction; both move wrong.

GOOD [operational definition -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Surplus distributed such that majority benefit is the primary
  outcome, with correction capacity intact and aimed at actual
  deviation.
  Three conditions:
    Boundary B populations bear costs proportional to benefit
      received [not disproportionate extraction].
    Correction capacity functional and distributed [not
      monopolized by Boundary A].
    Exit and correction options available to Boundary B [not
      structurally eliminated].
  Not perfectionism: good in the operational sense does not
  require equal outcomes or zero cost to anyone. It requires
  that the correction mechanism can fire against actual
  deviations and that majority populations are the primary
  beneficiaries of the system's surplus.
  Relationship to ascending path: good is not a fixed state
  but a trajectory -- toward more distributed surplus, more
  functional correction capacity, more available exit. Systems
  moving in this direction are good by operational definition
  regardless of current position.

C1 -- FUNDAMENTAL [innermost]
  Qualities: GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION /
    CORRECTION CAPACITY
  Function: takes in, transforms, corrects, returns.
  Self-contained at cellular minimum -- confirmed independently.
  Can run without C2 or C3. Pre-axial systems run C1 only.

C2 -- EPISTEMIC [wraps C1]
  Qualities: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY /
    REFERENCE INTEGRITY
  Function: makes C1 readable from inside and outside.
  C1 without C2 = blind generation. C2 without C1 = readable
  failure.
  Key: high-functioning C1 + degraded C2 = False Coherence
  signature. Most dangerous -- misread as high-functioning by
  C2-captured instruments.

C3 -- TEMPORAL [wraps C2]
  Qualities: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY /
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
  Function: extends the readable, correctable cycle across time.
  Without C3: each generation reinvents or inherits locked state.

INTERFACE [L3.5 -- unchanged]
  RECOGNITION / COMPATIBILITY / RECIPROCITY
  Contact protocol between independent three-cycle systems.
  Not a fourth wrapping cycle.

CYCLE INTERFERENCE PATTERNS [replaces cascade language]:
  C1 blind [C1 intact, C2 degraded]: high output, unreadable.
    False Coherence. Cases: Enron, WATF towers.
  C1 starved [C2 intact, C1 degraded]: readable failure.
    Correctable if substrate holds.
  C1 locked [C3 failed, C1/C2 intact]: correctable but non-
    transmissible. Each generation restarts correctly.
    Cases: Augustine vs Ignatius [T3.1 distinction].
  Full interference [all degraded]: terminal node. Containment
    only. Cases: Erebus, Oceania Party.

RECOVERY TRAJECTORIES [v16 -- predictive, falsifiable]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Each interference type produces a characteristic recovery
pattern when intervention occurs. Predictions with falsification
criteria. Confirmed findings in theory section: DIAGNOSTIC
COLLAPSE, C1 STARVED RECOVERY REQUIRES L3.5 CONTACT, L3.5
CONTACT TYPE MUST MATCH AXIS. Case library audit in Section
Four: RECOVERY CASE LIBRARY AUDIT.

  C1 BLIND RECOVERY [C2 restoration while C1 intact]
    Prediction: recovery is fast and discontinuous. C1 was
    running at full capacity throughout -- once C2 surface is
    honest, accumulated surplus becomes suddenly visible and
    correction fires immediately against backlog of unread
    deviations.
    Characteristic signature: sharp correction burst on C2
    restoration, not gradual improvement. System appears to
    deteriorate briefly as hidden deviations surface before
    stabilizing at higher function.
    Why: C1 was generating without readable output. All that
    generation is suddenly legible. Correction fires against
    everything it couldn't see. Looks like crisis; is actually
    triage.
    Intervention requirement: C2 instrument must be independent
    of the captured surface -- restoring C2 using the same
    instrument that was captured restores the capture, not the
    surface.
    Falsification: if C2 restoration produces gradual linear
    improvement rather than discontinuous burst followed by
    stabilization, C1 blind recovery prediction fails. Also
    falsified if system collapses on C2 restoration rather than
    correcting -- would indicate C1 was more degraded than the
    blind reading showed.
    Case candidates: post-Enron accounting reform [C2 surface
    restoration -- did correction fire in burst?]; Glasnost
    [Soviet C2 partial restoration -- information surface
    opened, correction burst followed].

  C1 STARVED RECOVERY [C1 restoration while C2 intact]
    Prediction: recovery is slow, substrate-dependent, and
    requires idle cycles before visible improvement begins.
    C2 is readable throughout -- the system knows exactly what
    is wrong and why. But knowing does not restore substrate.
    Characteristic signature: extended flat period with
    accurate self-diagnosis before output improvement begins.
    The flat period is not failure -- it is substrate
    regeneration prerequisite. Premature intervention pressure
    during the flat period restarts the depletion cycle.
    Why: C2 intact means the system can accurately report its
    own degradation. This is often misread as pessimism or
    dysfunction. It is accurate measurement. Correction cannot
    fire until C1 substrate crosses threshold -- no amount of
    epistemic clarity substitutes for generative capacity.
    Intervention requirement: protected idle cycles mandatory.
    External pressure to produce during recovery window depletes
    the substrate being restored. Counter-intuitive -- the
    correct intervention looks like doing less, not more.
    Falsification: if C1 starved recovery produces rapid
    improvement without idle cycle period, prediction fails.
    Also falsified if accurate self-diagnosis during flat period
    does not precede recovery -- would indicate C2 more degraded
    than starved reading showed.
    Case candidates: post-burnout individual recovery [known
    mechanism, documented flat period before function returns];
    post-collapse institutional rebuilding [Bell Labs
    dissolution aftermath -- did substrate recovery precede
    output recovery?].

  C1 LOCKED RECOVERY [C3 restoration while C1/C2 intact]
    Prediction: recovery requires generational patience and
    produces no visible improvement within the originating
    system. C1 and C2 functional throughout -- the system
    generates and reads correctly. The deficit is transmission.
    Characteristic signature: recovery visible only in
    successor systems, not in the originating system. The
    locked system may never visibly recover -- it continues
    functioning at C1/C2 level until it ends. Recovery is the
    successor generating a correctable transmission rather than
    inheriting the lock.
    Why: C3 failure is not a deficit in the system's current
    function -- it is a deficit in what the system passes
    forward. The originating system has nothing to recover.
    The successors have everything to gain. This is the only
    interference type where "recovery" is definitionally
    cross-generational.
    Intervention requirement: transmission method must be
    separated from transmission content. Successors need
    access to the generative procedure, not just the
    conclusions. Requires the originating system to transmit
    its own revision process, including its errors -- the
    hardest transmission for any system with identity
    investment in its conclusions.
    Falsification: if C1 locked recovery produces improvement
    in the originating system rather than in successors, or
    produces improvement within a single generation, prediction
    fails. Also falsified if method transmission without
    conclusion transmission produces Fertile Lock in successors
    anyway -- would indicate C3 failure is deeper than
    transmission selectivity alone.
    Case candidates: Benedictine reform cycles [method
    transmitted, successor revision documented across
    generations]; Socratic transmission through Plato through
    Aristotle [each revised, each produced distinct output].

  FULL INTERFERENCE RECOVERY [all cycles degraded]
    Prediction: internal recovery structurally unavailable.
    Any apparent internal recovery is False Coherence
    formation -- a new C2 capture producing the appearance
    of recovery while extraction continues or accelerates.
    Characteristic signature: recovery claims from inside full
    interference state are the highest-probability weapon
    deployment in the framework. The system that announces
    its own recovery under full interference is deploying
    Coherence Mimicry, not recovering.
    Why: full interference means C1 generates without surplus,
    C2 reads without honesty, C3 transmits the captured state.
    Every internal mechanism available for recovery is already
    running on corrupted substrate and captured surface. There
    is no internal leverage point. External contact is the
    only available intervention -- and external contact
    requires the external system to have sufficient
    COMPATIBILITY to engage without being pulled into the
    extraction interface.
    Intervention requirement: external actor with independent
    C2 surface and sufficient TENSION MAINTENANCE to hold
    structure under contact. Containment if no such actor
    available. Attempted recovery using captured internal
    mechanisms accelerates full interference -- gives the
    extraction architecture a new legitimacy surface.
    Falsification: if a system in confirmed full interference
    produces genuine C2 restoration from inside without
    external intervention, full interference recovery
    prediction fails. This would require revising the terminal
    state classification.
    No confirmed case of this in current library.
    Case candidates [containment confirmed, not recovery]:
    Erebus [FF14 -- external intervention only exit]; Room
    101 [1984 -- no recovery pathway confirmed].

RECOVERY TRAJECTORY APPLICATION RULE:
  Identify interference type before prescribing intervention.
  Wrong intervention for type accelerates degradation:
    C1 blind: pressure to produce more = accelerates C1
      depletion before C2 is restored.
    C1 starved: demand for accurate reporting = correct but
      useless without substrate restoration first.
    C1 locked: urgency about current output = misses the
      transmission deficit entirely.
    Full interference: internal reform programs = new capture
      surface, not recovery.
  Triage follows interference type, not coherence score alone.

ENRON RESOLUTION [no longer requires probability gradient patch]:
  C2 attacked directly without prior C3 failure. C1 ran hot.
  Cycle interference model handles this natively.

SCALE RECURRENCE [confirmed structural property]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  A quality's structure reappears at multiple nesting depths
  with the same logic but different scope and function. Not a
  merger -- each instance operates independently at its depth.
  Confirmed instances:
    CORRECTION CAPACITY: C1 tension unit + constant operating
      at all depths. Same correction logic at both individual
      depth and meta depth simultaneously.
    Reciprocal exchange [T1.4 / L3.5]: GENERATIVE CYCLE
      substrate at social scale [T1.4] and full inter-system
      tension unit at L3.5 [RECOGNITION/COMPATIBILITY/
      RECIPROCITY]. Same structure, different scope -- one
      sustains cycles, one enables system connection.
    CYCLE STABILITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [T1.5 / T2.1]: timing
      integrity at L1 [rhythm] and historical legibility at
      L2 [past corrections retrievable]. Same persistence-of-
      pattern structure at different tower levels.
  Implication: scale recurrence predicts that any quality
  operating at L1 may have a structural analog at higher
  levels. When a test at one level feels like it overlaps
  another level, check for scale recurrence before merging
  -- the levels may be doing the same thing at different
  scopes.

  SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
     [Tower: L3]
     Successor's capacity to revise transmitted content
     against new evidence without losing transmission fidelity
     to founding generative principle.
     High = correctable inheritance [revises conclusions,
     preserves method]. Low = Fertile Lock [cannot revise
     without losing identity].
     Distinct from TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY: TRANSMISSION
     SELECTIVITY measures what is sent. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
     measures successor's capacity to work with what was
     received.
     Primary proof: Benedictine [high -- correctable
     transmission] vs Dominican [low -- lock into Aquinas].
     Same transmission context, different outcomes.
     Test: successor revises without losing founding principle?

  CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
     [Tower: L3 + FOUNDATION]
     Axis traceable to actual outputs not just internal
     conviction or stated orientation. GENUINE AXIS asks
     whether axis was internally generated and examined.
     CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING asks whether that axis is anchored
     to real-world causal outputs.
     Distinct from CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
     anchors correction to actor. CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
     anchors axis to reality.
     Failure: Sincere Delusion -- genuine examined axis with
     zero connection to actual outputs. Axis real to the
     system; not real to the world it claims to act on.
     Primary proof: Maiad [genuine axis, not grounded in
     achievable outputs]; cargo cults [sincere axis, no causal
     connection].
     Test: axis anchored to actual outputs?

  MORAL LEGIBILITY [WIP 15 -- see WIP THEORY STAGING section]


POPULATION COHERENCE CEILING [promoted from extended theory]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Most humans may be Functional Structural Coherence inside
  artificially maintained environments. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
  appears present but formed entirely within controlled
  conditions -- indistinguishable internally.
  Government as environmental controller: intentional [axis
  formation window targeted] or side effect [MEASUREMENT
  INTEGRITY failure by institutional diffusion].
  Hard implication: genuine CORRECTION CAPACITY at population
  scale structurally rare. Bottleneck: Axial population
  precondition for producing more Axial individuals. System
  selects against transition automatically.
  Operational ceiling: Mixed Coherence -- sufficient
  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY/CAUSAL LEGIBILITY to detect and
  resist weapon deployment while accepting lower SURPLUS
  DISTRIBUTION ceiling and constant extraction pressure. Not
  a compromise -- only stable configuration under current
  conditions. True coherence remains measurement ceiling,
  not operational target.
  Circular dependency: conditions required for True coherence
  at scale only producible by True coherence at scale.
  Unresolvable without external intervention.

  EXTERNAL CONFIRMATION [Core Human Competence -- Weinhagen/
  NLI]: CHC framework independently arrives at same population
  ceiling finding. Cooperation Competence Hypothesis: stable
  cooperation at scale only possible where sufficient
  proportion of individuals possess capacities to refuse
  participation without catastrophic personal cost. Without
  genuine refusal capacity, cooperation = compliance.
  Four CHC domains map to framework qualities:
    Physiological [food/water/shelter/defense] = GENERATIVE
      CYCLE
    Psychological [regulation under pressure] = CORRECTION
      CAPACITY
    Cognitive [observe/orient/decide/act] = GENUINE AXIS
    Perceptual [situational awareness] = MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
  Anxiety-as-accurate-signal: EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION works
  because threat overestimation amplifies real GENERATIVE
  CYCLE vulnerability. Suppressing the signal = taking
  batteries out of smoke alarm.
  CHC hostile suppression finding: competence loss actively
  maintained by systems benefiting from dependency. CHC treats
  this as secondary; framework reads it as primary [SUCCESSOR
  AUTONOMY Suppression Structural Limit -- weapon stack
  systematically prevents SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY development].

COHERENCE CORRUPTION [structural limit on scale]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Anti-coherence has three distinct methods against emerging
  coherence:
  1. NOISE [loud]: saturate the signal environment so coherent
     signals cannot be distinguished from incoherent ones.
     MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY capture by volume not capture. No
     monopolist required -- sufficient noise level disables
     correction navigation regardless of source.
  2. CORRUPTION [infiltration]: insert extraction architecture
     inside coherent structures before they reach scale.
     Formation Capture, Axis Substitution, Correction Monopoly.
     Coherence appears to grow but the generative mechanism is
     replaced before outputs confirm it.
  3. DESTRUCTION [direct]: eliminate coherent actors or
     structures when noise and corruption fail. Highest cost,
     most visible, last resort. Malcolm X, Merton [suspicious
     timing], historical pattern of high-coherence actors
     dying at transition moments.
  Sequence: noise first [cheapest], corruption when noise
  insufficient, destruction when corruption fails. Each step
  more visible and costly.
  Scale barrier: coherence at small scale is tolerated because
  contained. When coherent structures approach scale sufficient
  to affect the extraction architecture, the method escalates.
  The barrier is not fixed -- it is proportional to the threat
  the coherence poses.
  Result: coherence at scale requires surviving all three
  methods simultaneously. Individual coherence is achievable.
  Small-group coherence is achievable. Institutional coherence
  at scale that threatens extraction architecture has not
  historically survived all three methods applied in sequence.
  This is the structural reason the Population Coherence
  Ceiling holds: not only is coherence rare to form, it is
  actively prevented from reaching scale by a self-maintaining
  suppression architecture.
  Survival condition: coherence that survives all three methods
  does so by operating outside the normal visibility surface.
  Silent, effective, and outside the channels the suppression
  architecture monitors. High-coherence structures that
  announce themselves or operate through captured channels get
  noise/corruption/destruction applied in sequence. Those that
  don't announce themselves and don't need captured
  infrastructure to function are structurally harder to target.
  Linux/open source partially confirmed this -- decentralized,
  low-capture-surface, outputs speak before the structure is
  legible enough to corrupt. The survival method is not
  resistance to the three methods -- it is structural
  inaccessibility to them.
  See: SILENT ALTERNATIVE [coherence type] -- anti-capture by
  structural design, not defensive resistance.
  Force-reveal problem: true silence may be impossible at
  sufficient scale. Evil must destroy [extraction architecture
  threatened]; not-evil must destroy [anything outside their
  measurement surface reads as illegitimate by REFERENCE
  INTEGRITY capture]. Both produce identical pressure.
  Costly removal as viable alternative: if true silence
  impossible, raise the cost of the four-condition test
  against yourself -- make conditions 2 and 4 fail for the
  attacker. Not invisible; too expensive to remove. Same
  structural protection, different mechanism.
  Coherence is not good: Silent Alternative and costly-removal
  structures work for any axis. Coherent extraction is still
  coherent by the tower. Framework is descriptive not
  prescriptive.

  FOURTH PHASE [v16 -- when noise, corruption, destruction
    fail]:
    If all three methods fail against a coherent structure,
    the extraction architecture has no remaining playbook.
    What happens next is not determined by the framework --
    it is self-determined by the coherent actor based on
    conditions at that moment.
    Framework cannot prescribe the fourth phase because:
      It is context-specific [what is possible depends on
        the specific state of the extraction architecture,
        the coherent actor's substrate, and the available
        options at that moment].
      Prescribing it in advance would make it visible and
        targetable -- the fourth phase survives by not being
        a known template.
      The actor who reaches it has survived three escalating
        methods; their judgment about conditions is more
        reliable than any prior specification.
    What the framework can say: the fourth phase is not
      revenge, not destruction, and not capture of the
      extraction architecture's own tools. Those paths route
      back into the extraction logic.
    The direction is the only constraint: toward correction
      capacity, distributed surplus, available exit. How
      that is expressed given specific conditions is the
      actor's determination, not the framework's.
  Relative coherence matters: less extractive with some
  CORRECTION CAPACITY beats maximum extraction even when
  neither is good. The spectrum is real. Not a binary.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every system sits on a spectrum between pure generation and
  pure extraction. Neither terminal stable.
  Tower reading: range point = hole size relative to wall
  thickness at L1. Tension field = force holding ratio stable.
  Weapon implication: most efficient attack removes corrective
  pole [CORRECTION CAPACITY at L1] rather than maximizing
  extraction directly. Removing CORRECTION CAPACITY shifts
  range point toward extraction without requiring increased
  extraction effort. See Tower Codependence Model L1.

EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational -- structural finding -- v17]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Pattern confirmed: extraction accumulates past substrate
  floor -> somatic correction fires at population scale
  [uncoordinated, distributed] -> existing structure
  destabilizes or collapses -> new formation occurs, often
  reproducing extraction architecture at lower intensity
  initially -> extraction gradient climbs again.
  This is not Evil exit. It is managed oscillation around the
  extraction axis. Each cycle resets extraction to a lower
  initial rate; the architecture that produces extraction is
  not removed, only stressed and restarted.

  WHY CYCLE REPEATS [structural mechanism]:
    New formation occurs under C1 starved conditions.
    Formation Capture fires into the new structure before L4
    can transmit correction methodology.
    Successors inherit corrected C1 without correction
    architecture -- Protestant Reformation pattern at
    civilizational scale. C1 rebuilds; C3 transmits the
    corrected state, not the correction method.
    Result: next cycle starts with cleaner substrate and the
    same extractable architecture.

  EVIL EXIT [genuine -- distinct from cycle reset]:
    Evil exit requires: correction methodology transmitted
    across the formation event [C3 functional at collapse
    boundary] AND new structure formed on different axis,
    not lower extraction rate on same axis.
    No confirmed case at civilizational scale in library.
    Why no confirmed case: collapse events destroy C3
    preferentially. The complexity that transmits methodology
    is the first substrate extraction consumes. Bronze Age,
    Western Rome: methodology destroyed before structure
    collapses. Highest-coherence individuals exit early or
    are absorbed. Remaining population forms under C1 starved
    conditions -- L3.5 contact requirement applies at
    civilizational scale but axis-matching is structurally
    unavailable when the contact pool is also depleted.
    [Honest limit: this is the framework's own civilizational
    prognosis. T8 applies. Track against Section Five
    evidence independently.]

  SOMATIC CORRECTION MECHANISM [why each cycle fires]:
    Population-scale somatic firing is distributed and
    uncoordinated. High force, low CAUSAL LEGIBILITY.
    Correction fires accurately [extraction is real];
    correction cannot aim [chain illegible at scale].
    Result: system destabilizes without directed correction.
    New formation fills the vacuum; extraction architecture
    reassembles because it is the available template.
    Somatic forcing is genuine correction, not rebellion
    or politics. It fires when substrate depletion crosses
    the survival threshold. The timing is substrate-driven,
    not ideologically organized. This is why it is hard to
    suppress entirely -- it does not require ideology,
    coordination, or leadership. It requires enough people
    simultaneously hitting the floor.

  GOOD PEOPLE AS CONSTANT:
    The majority of the population attempting exit from
    extraction at any given time is structurally confirmed
    by the cycle itself. Each reset phase contains genuine
    correction attempts. The attempts fail to escape the
    cycle not because they are weak but because C3 is
    destroyed at the collapse boundary. The exit attempts
    are real. The transmission problem is structural.
    Framework implication: the question is not whether
    people want good [they do, at majority; confirmed by
    cycle pattern]. The question is whether correction
    methodology can be transmitted across the collapse
    boundary. That is the C3 problem, not the C1 or human-
    nature problem.

  DECLINING SUBSTRATE PREDICTION:
    If extraction rate increases faster than formation can
    stabilize per cycle, cycles shorten. Shorter cycles =
    less time for C3 transmission = lower floor per cycle.
    Trajectory under this condition is declining substrate,
    not stable oscillation.
    Falsification: documented cycle where correction
    methodology [not just C1 restoration] crossed the
    formation boundary. No such case in library.
    Alternative falsification: stable or lengthening cycles
    despite accelerating extraction rate. Would indicate
    C3 transmission mechanism more robust than predicted.

ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [confirmed structural finding -- v18]
[S:4 -- E4: HRE + Cistercian/Jesuit + Extraction Cycle civilizational
  multi-domain; M4: cost accounting confirmed differentially across
  HRE centralization attempts; F3: falsification tested against
  Protestant Reformation [generation won once, transmitted method
  partially -- conclusions only; but anti-coherence did not achieve
  permanent suppression despite 300 years of institutional pressure].
  WIP cleared v18. Promoted from candidate to confirmed.]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Anti-coherence must win continuously. Generation only has
  to win once and transmit.

  Extraction requires:
    Ongoing suppression cost across all three Coherence
    Corruption methods [Noise -> Corruption -> Destruction].
    Each method more expensive and visible than the last.
    Destruction is highest-cost, highest-visibility, last
    resort. All three methods must succeed continuously --
    a single failure at any stage allows transmission.

  Generation requires:
    One correctable transmission crossing one generational
    boundary.
    The substrate that receives it does not have to be the
    substrate that produced it.
    Transmission content does not have to survive in the
    original form -- method survival is sufficient [see C1
    LOCKED RECOVERY; L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION].

  Why this is structurally load-bearing:
    Extraction terminates its own substrate [see EXTRACTION
    CYCLE]. The maintenance cost of suppression increases
    as the substrate being suppressed depletes.
    Generation is self-funding when transmission succeeds --
    each correctable successor reduces the required
    maintenance cost of the entire chain.
    HRE primary confirmation: 800 years of distributed
    correction capacity held because centralization cost
    exceeded extraction yield at every step. Napoleon
    dissolved it in 1806 -- but the methodological template
    had already transmitted. That template is why any
    correctable analytical work is possible downstream.

  Stress-test [v18 -- alternative explanations examined]:
    ALT 1: HRE held because of geography and military
      stalemate, not cost asymmetry.
      Against: Holy Roman Emperors had repeated military
      superiority at specific moments [Frederick II,
      Charles V]. Did not convert to centralization.
      Cost accounting predicts this -- extraction yield
      from a fragmented substrate was higher than the
      yield from a unified one under sustained suppression
      cost. Military capacity present; conversion not
      attempted because unprofitable. Cost asymmetry
      explanation holds where geography/military does not.
    ALT 2: Protestant Reformation confirms extraction
      won, not generation -- Catholicism was suppressed.
      Against: the finding is asymmetric. Catholicism
      is not the generation case. The generation case
      is: method transmission [any correctable successor
      tradition] survived despite 300 years of
      institutional pressure from multiple directions
      [Catholic Counter-Reformation, Protestant
      Confessionalization, state church capture]. The
      point is not that generation always wins the
      dominant cultural position -- it is that permanent
      suppression is structurally unavailable. The
      Reformation confirms this: anti-coherence applied
      maximum available force; correctable transmission
      survived anyway. S4 confirmation stands.
    ALT 3: Extraction Cycle falsification condition
      [declining substrate prediction] could falsify
      this finding if substrate doesn't decline.
      Against: these are related but separate findings.
      EXTRACTION CYCLE predicts substrate depletion.
      ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY predicts that
      generation survives suppression attempts. They
      share the terminal state [extraction consumes
      its own substrate] but the asymmetry finding
      holds even before terminal state is reached.
      Falsification of EXTRACTION CYCLE would weaken
      the "extraction has no stable terminal state"
      claim but would not falsify the cost structure
      during the active suppression window.
  Stress-test result: alternative explanations examined;
    none overturn. S4 rating confirmed. Promote to
    confirmed structural finding.

  Falsification condition [stated v17, confirmed v18]:
    Finding is falsified if: documented multi-generational
    suppression campaign achieves permanent elimination
    of correctable transmission -- no successor tradition,
    no methodological survival, no downstream continuation
    anywhere in the substrate. Zero-survival outcome
    over 3+ generations with documented suppression
    effort. No confirmed case in library. Every
    documented suppression with adequate evidence shows
    partial or full transmission survival.

  Operational implication:
    Anti-coherence wins most engagements. It does not need
    to win all of them. But the cost structure means it
    cannot achieve permanent suppression without consuming
    the substrate it needs to sustain suppression. The
    endpoint of total success is total extraction, which
    terminates the system producing the suppression.
    Extraction has no stable terminal state. Generation
    does [correctable transmission achieved].

  Cross-ref: COHERENCE CORRUPTION [three methods];
    EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational]; ANTI-TOWER
    [confirmed structural inverse]; HRE SUBSIDIARITY
    [cost-accounting confirmation in Section Five];
    FOURTH PHASE [when all three methods fail];
    WIP 14 [L4.5 -- generation only needs to win once
    applies to civilizational transmission threshold;
    anti-capture architecture requirement follows from
    cost asymmetry logic].

CONVERGENT DISCOVERY [promoted from extended theory]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Framework accuracy across independent domains too consistent
  to be noise. Three explanations:
  [1] Convergent discovery -- qualities describe load-bearing
      structure.
  [2] Anthropic selection -- only coherent systems persist long
      enough to be studied.
  [3] Design -- upstream agency.
  [1] and [2] sufficient. [3] indistinguishable from [1] at
  the limit. Finding: following coherence path finds a shape
  already there. Framework follows coherent systems; they
  don't follow it.

MINIMAL UNIFICATION [confirmed structural pattern]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  A single structural fact that makes a large class of
  previously scattered results coherent. Distinct from CAUSAL
  LEGIBILITY restoration [making one chain legible] -- minimal
  unification makes many chains legible simultaneously via
  single anchor.
  Confirmed instances:
    Compressed sensing [Candès]: signal sparsity as the
      structural fact explaining accurate recovery from
      incomplete data. Previously separate results in signal
      processing, medical imaging, statistics unified under
      one principle.
    Unique Games Conjecture [Khot]: if confirmed, unique games
      hardness as the structural fact explaining approximation
      barriers across optimization theory. Dozens of
      independent hardness results would collapse to one
      cause.
  Framework parallel: same move the framework makes --
  identifying the minimal structural fact [coherence
  qualities] that makes diverse system behaviors legible
  under one architecture.
  Implication: when scattered results resist unification, the
  missing element is usually not more data -- it is the
  identification of the correct minimal structural anchor.
  REFERENCE INTEGRITY application: fragmented reference
  standards [one for each result] collapse to one calibration
  anchor. Measurement becomes dramatically more efficient.
  Cognitive confirmation [1,200-player experiment, Phys.org,
  May 2026]: People who understood how concepts connect
  invented more novel solutions than those with the same
  facts without connection structure. Structural knowledge
  was the generative variable, not fact volume. CAUSAL AXIS
  GROUNDING at creative scale -- knowing which structure
  applies is the prior that makes facts generative. Confirms:
  minimal unification is not just an abstract mathematical
  property -- it is the cognitive architecture of invention.
  Most common path principle: the most traveled path through
  a problem space is not necessarily the best path -- it is
  the path with the lowest accumulated friction, which
  optimizes for ease not output quality. Structural
  understanding enables non-obvious paths that common
  traversal never surfaces. High-volume use of a path =
  REFERENCE INTEGRITY capture of "how this is done." Breaks
  when the structural map is read instead of the usage
  pattern.

SMART VS STUPID EXTRACTION [revised -- extraction always wrong]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Previous framing: smart extraction [below innovation
  threshold] vs stupid extraction [past threshold]. Revised:
  both are wrong. Smart extraction is slower destruction --
  the threshold is still crossed, just later. Smart death is
  still death.
  The distinction that matters: predation and competition
  [honest boundary, visible conflict] vs extraction [captured
  measurement surface, hidden mechanism]. Speed of destruction
  is irrelevant to the structural judgment.
  Explanatory residue retained: some systems generated genuine
  surplus despite extraction overhead [Song Dynasty, Bell
  Labs] because extraction floor existed below which
  suppression became unprofitable. This explains the
  mechanism without endorsing it.
  See: EXTRACTION WITH LIMITS VS CENTRALIZED EXTRACTION
  [updated].

EXTRACTION WITH LIMITS VS CENTRALIZED EXTRACTION [revised]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Both are extraction -- both wrong. Distinction is speed and
  legibility, not moral category. Centralized is faster
  destruction; distributed with limits is slower destruction.
  Useful structural distinction retained: centralized
  extraction fails faster [condition 2 absent, no cross-
  correction]. Distributed with limits sustains longer but
  endpoint is the same.
  Confirmation: mit'a -> encomienda = slow destruction to
  fast destruction. Not slow destruction as acceptable
  alternative.
  Framework position: predation and competition are the honest
  alternatives. Extraction in any form is captured
  measurement.

TOPOLOGY FINDINGS + DISTRIBUTED CAPTURE NETWORK [merged]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every sustained evil: consumption-dominant terminal node
  fed by capture chains through intermediate nodes that
  believe they operate normally. Highest surplus under
  external pressure; lowest during dominance. Transition =
  highest-value measurement point.
  Distributed Capture Network type: nodes believe independent,
  terminal extraction point invisible from inside, correction
  and extraction share identical infrastructure. Mont Pelerin
  [complexity as illegibility], WEF [complexity as
  legitimacy]. Same Fertile Lock trajectory, different optics.

ANTI-TOWER [confirmed structural inverse]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Anti-coherence has predictable structure operating as tower
  inverse. Noise -> Corruption -> Destruction sequence; weapon
  stack [MSC + ECD + Calibration Strip + Formation Capture];
  SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY suppression as primary mechanism. Not
  chaos -- inverted tower with consistent function.
  Unfalsifiability as confirmation: if anti-tower were absent,
  counter-attempts would succeed. The fact that every counter-
  attempt routes through the same mechanisms -- noise
  suppresses signal, corruption captures the counter-movement,
  destruction targets what survives -- is the signature of a
  functioning structure. The inability to disprove the anti-
  tower is proof of the anti-tower.
  T8 partial: recursive closure is the anti-tower working as
  designed, not a measurement failure. Does not void the

PREDATION / COMPETITION / EXTRACTION [fundamental distinction]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Predation: honest violence. Boundary visible. The prey
  knows the relationship. A lion kills. Does not build a
  farm, keep prey mostly alive, and call it efficient
  resource management.
  Competition: honest conflict. Boundary visible. Both
  parties know the terms. Zero-sum possible but declared.
  Extraction: trickery as foundation. Requires prey to
  misunderstand the relationship, cooperate with its own
  consumption, and call it beneficial. Measurement surface
  capture is the mechanism.
  Slightly wrong is still wrong -- smart extraction is slower
  destruction. The innovation threshold is still crossed,
  just later. Smart death is still death. The crime is not
  the degree; it is the lie that makes cooperation possible.
  Framework: predation and competition are structurally
  honest. Extraction is not. Politics at scale = extraction
  [stealing] not predation -- requires the legitimacy surface
  that predation doesn't need.

MEASUREMENT-ACTION UNITY [framework-derived principle]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Measurement that produces no action = observation captured
  by the observer. Action without measurement = force without
  calibration -- extraction's operating mode.
  Legitimate conflict [predation, competition]: measurement
  drives action, both visible to parties bearing the cost.
  Extraction: action separated from honest measurement.
  Requires the gap to function -- prey must not see the
  measurement surface.
  Test: is the measurement driving the action visible to the
  party bearing the cost? If no, extraction.

IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT [promoted from science appendix]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  SUBSTRATE REGENERATION requires idle cycles. Default mode
  network [consolidation, memory integration, meaning-making]
  fires only in attention gaps. Continuous input extracts the
  recovery window before it can fire. Exhaustion is SR deficit
  not overwork.
  Applies at all scales: individual [attention gap
  elimination], institutional [no recovery time between
  correction cycles], civilizational [extraction past the
  regeneration rate].
  Test: does the system have protected idle cycles? If no,
  SR is being extracted.


DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [confirmed case type -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Cross-ref: RECOVERY TRAJECTORIES [cycle model -- predictive
  detail].
  C2 restoration in a system where C1 is already terminal
  produces collapse rather than recovery. The collapse is the
  correct outcome -- accurate diagnosis of a terminal state
  the False Coherence surface had concealed.
  Mechanism: False Coherence surface masks terminal C1
  trajectory. C2 partial restoration makes accumulated
  deviations simultaneously legible. Correction burst fires.
  C1 cannot absorb correction load -- already terminal.
  Collapse follows.
  Distinct from:
    C1 blind recovery failure [C1 was recoverable --
      correction burst should produce stabilization;
      Diagnostic Collapse means C1 was never recoverable
      before C2 opened].
    Simple collapse [no C2 restoration -- C1 just fails].
    Coherence Mimicry [false C2 restoration -- new surface
      capture].
  Confirmed cases:
    GLASNOST [primary]: Soviet C1 terminal 15+ years before
      Glasnost. C2 partial opening made terminal state
      legible. Collapse = correct diagnosis. Not recovery
      failure.
    ARAB_SPRING_DIFFERENTIAL [Libya]: 42-year deliberate C1
      destruction. C2 opening via protest + NATO. Diagnostic
      Collapse confirmed by paired comparison with Tunisia
      [C1 recoverable, same C2 opening, recovery trajectory].
  Intervention implication: C1 substrate audit required
    before any C2 restoration intervention. Opening C2 on
    terminal C1 is irreversible -- correction burst fires
    regardless of whether C1 can absorb it. External actors
    [NATO, sanctions, information operations] enabling C2
    opening without C1 audit produce Diagnostic Collapse or
    False Coherence reformation.
  Status: CONFIRMED [two primary cases + controlled
  comparison]. S4.

C1 STARVED RECOVERY REQUIRES L3.5 CONTACT [provisional
  finding -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  C1 starved recovery does not occur through individual
  correction capacity alone. External relational substrate
  via L3.5 COMPATIBILITY contact is the structural
  precondition.
  Confirmed across five independent cases:
    Farnese [Berserk]: relational contact with Guts/Schierke.
    Zuko [Avatar]: Iroh as substrate source restored.
    Joshua Graham [Fallout NV]: New Canaan community contact.
    Wittgenstein [real]: Cambridge re-engagement 1929
      preceded Investigations revision. Isolation [1920-1926]
      produced zero revision despite C2 intact. Near-control
      condition.
    Le Guin [real]: feminist community engagement preceded
      documented gender self-corrections.
  No counterexample in library.
  Mechanism: C1 substrate too depleted for internal
  generation to restart. External contact provides substrate
  that internal generation cannot supply until above
  threshold. L3.5 COMPATIBILITY is the delivery mechanism --
  not willpower, insight, or accurate self-diagnosis. Contact
  is the substrate source.
  Status: PROVISIONAL. S3.

L3.5 CONTACT TYPE MUST MATCH INSTRUMENT FRICTION AXIS
  [v16 extension]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Contact type varies across all five recovery cases. What
  is consistent is not type but structural fit: the contact
  that fired recovery matched the target instrument's primary
  receivable friction axis. Mismatched contact provides L3.5
  presence without substrate transfer.

  CASE MAPPING:
    Farnese: primary axis = sensory/somatic. Contact type =
      physical danger + combat survival + bodily co-presence
      with Guts. Logical argument or emotional reassurance
      would not have reached her instrument. Somatic contact
      did. Evidence: recovery fires during physical ordeal
      episodes, not during conversations.

    Zuko: primary axis = social/relational. Contact type =
      Iroh's unconditional presence, patience, warmth without
      correction pressure. Iroh never argued Zuko into
      correction; he waited. Evidence: intellectual argument
      from other characters [Katara, Aang] produced no
      recovery. Iroh's relational contact did. Same
      information content, different axis.

    Joshua Graham: primary axis = logical/social hybrid.
      Contact type = structured community with coherent
      meaning framework [New Canaan + scripture + Burned Man
      narrative]. Not pure emotional support; the community
      provided both belonging AND interpretive structure.
      Both axes required. Evidence: isolation post-Legion
      destruction did not produce recovery; community contact
      at New Canaan did.

    Wittgenstein: primary axis = logical/systemic. Contact
      type = adversarial intellectual friction [Vienna Circle,
      Russell, Sraffa's specific gesture challenge]. Not
      emotional support -- he had social stability in Austria,
      produced nothing. Cambridge intellectual challenge
      fired correction. Evidence: the isolation period had
      social contact [village community] but zero
      logical/systemic friction. Cambridge provided logical
      friction; revision began. Key case: Sraffa's reportedly
      single adversarial gesture challenge [pointing to
      Neapolitan chin flick, asking "what is the logical form
      of that?"] may have been the specific trigger. Single
      contact event on correct axis.

    Le Guin: primary axis = logical/social hybrid [writer +
      intellectual]. Contact type = feminist community of
      practice -- relational belonging + intellectual
      challenge to prior work simultaneously. Both axes
      active; both required.

  STRUCTURAL FINDING [v16]:
    L3.5 contact type must match target instrument's primary
    receivable friction axis. Three axes confirmed relevant:
      Sensory/somatic: physical co-presence, shared bodily
        risk, survival-level stakes. Not reducible to
        emotional warmth.
      Social/relational: unconditional presence, consistent
        availability, warmth without agenda. Not intellectual
        challenge.
      Logical/systemic: adversarial intellectual contact from
        peer who takes work seriously enough to contest it.
        Not social support.
    Mismatch: contact on wrong axis provides L3.5 presence
      [counts toward contact requirement] but does not
      transfer substrate. Explains failed recovery attempts:
      therapeutic approaches aimed at wrong axis for
      instrument type.

  INTERVENTION IMPLICATION [updated]:
    Correct intervention is not just relational contact --
    it is contact on the axis the target instrument can
    receive. Prescribing social/emotional support for a
    logical/systemic instrument [Wittgenstein type] provides
    presence without substrate transfer. Prescribing
    intellectual challenge for a somatic instrument [Farnese
    type] same failure. Assessment required: identify
    target's primary receivable axis before prescribing
    contact type.

  FALSIFICATION CRITERIA [updated]:
    1. C1 starved recovery without L3.5 contact [original].
    2. C1 starved recovery via L3.5 contact on demonstrably
       mismatched axis -- would falsify axis-matching
       requirement.
    No such case in current library.

  STATUS: PROVISIONAL extended [five contact type mappings,
  axis-matching pattern consistent, no falsification].
  Promote axis-matching to confirmed when: independent case
  confirms recovery fired on matched axis AND failed on
  mismatched axis for same instrument within one case window.

SUBSTRATE REGENERATION requires idle cycles. Default mode
  network [consolidation, memory integration, meaning-making]
  fires only in attention gaps. Continuous input extracts the
  recovery window before it can fire. Exhaustion is SR deficit
  not overwork. Applies at all scales: individual [attention
  gap elimination], institutional [no recovery time between
  correction cycles], civilizational [extraction past the
  regeneration rate]. Test: does the system have protected
  idle cycles? If no, SR is being extracted.

SPEED AS ERROR SIGNAL [framework-derived principle]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The faster a solution is implemented the more likely it is
  wrong. Speed compresses the correction cycle -- insufficient
  time for MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY to verify, CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
  to trace, or CORRECTION CAPACITY to fire before the action
  lands. Best solutions are slow and careful: each step
  generates feedback that calibrates the next. Speed
  eliminates the feedback loop.
  Aggression and violence as reaction not initiation: if force
  is required it should fire as T1.3 [proportional response
  to detected deviation], not as primary axis expression.
  Initiated aggression = CORRECTION CAPACITY misfired before
  the deviation is confirmed. The asymmetry: reactive force
  has a measured target; initiated force creates the target
  retroactively. One is correction; one is weapon.

INSTITUTIONAL AXIS-MATCHING REQUIREMENT [provisional -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The contact-type-must-match-friction-axis finding
  [individual scale, C1 starved recovery] extends to
  institutional scale. Institutional C1 starved recovery:
  correction operating on a different axis than the
  dysfunction does not transfer substrate. Confirmed across
  four institutional recovery cases:

  Cistercian [organizational axis]:
    Cluny dysfunction = structural centralization producing
    wealth. Cistercian correction = organizational redesign
    [general chapter + local autonomy]. Same structural/
    organizational axis. Recovery: confirmed [L4, 700-year
    record].

  Jesuit [contemplative/individual axis]:
    Catholic spiritual dysfunction = external rule-following
    without internal examination. Jesuit correction = Examen
    [internal examination procedure]. Same contemplative
    axis. Recovery: confirmed [L4, 480-year record].

  Vatican II [doctrinal/methodological axis]:
    Counter-Reformation dysfunction = methodological lock
    [Neo-scholasticism]. Vatican II correction = historical
    method [nouvelle théologie on same methodological axis].
    Recovery: confirmed [C1 locked recovery primary case].

  China 1978 [split-axis case -- confirms by differential]:
    Maoist dysfunction = ideological axis [class struggle
    doctrine]. Economic correction = productive axis
    [matching C1 substrate]. Recovery: CONFIRMED for
    economic C1. Political correction = different axis [C2
    surface, not matching Maoist ideological axis]. Recovery:
    FAILED [Tiananmen]. Same system, same three conditions,
    axis-match determines which component recovered. Split
    case is the strongest institutional confirmation.

  FINDING [provisional]:
    Institutional correction must operate on the same
    functional axis as the dysfunction to transfer substrate.
    Cross-axis correction produces activity without substrate
    transfer -- institutionally equivalent to wrong-axis
    L3.5 contact. China 1978 provides controlled comparison:
    same system, same enabling moment, matched-axis correction
    succeeded, mismatched-axis correction failed.

  Falsification: documented institutional recovery via
    correction operating on demonstrably different axis than
    dysfunction. No such case in current library.

L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION [confirmed -- three cases + one
  failure]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Prediction: any institution transmitting conclusions without
  an embedded correction procedure [methodology as structure]
  will show Generational Reset within 2-3 generations.

  EVIDENCE BASE:
    Confirmed [methodology transmitted as structure]:
      Cistercian: general chapter + visitation as structure.
        Generational Reset broken. 700-year record.
      Jesuit: Examen as mandatory daily procedure.
        Generational Reset broken. 480-year record.
      Framework: addition filter + version convention.
        Generational Reset broken across 17 versions.
    Failed [conclusions transmitted, methodology absent]:
      Protestant Reformation: doctrinal conclusions
        transmitted. Generational Reset confirmed. ~40,000
        denominations.
    Partial [mixed transmission]:
      Bell Labs: research culture transmitted, correction
        methodology [let go when exciting] not embedded as
        structure. Generational Reset confirmed in all
        successors.
      China 1978: economic methodology partially embedded
        [agricultural reform procedure]; political correction
        methodology not embedded. Economic: holds. Political:
        Reset ongoing.

  ACTIVE TEST CASES [falsification candidates]:
    1. Open source software projects: methodology embedded
       as contribution procedures and code review processes
       [FOSS norms as structure]. Prediction: projects with
       embedded review methodology persist; those transmitting
       only codebase show Generational Reset on maintainer
       turnover. Timeline: 2-5 years per generation [fast
       test].
       STATUS [v16 CONFIRMED]: Linux kernel confirmed.
       Contribution Maturity Model, maintainer succession
       documentation [Linux 6.19, Jan 2026], patch review
       hierarchy all transmit methodology as embedded
       structure. Contributor retention excellent [Linux
       Foundation data]. Generational Reset not occurring.
       L4 PROPAGATION confirmed for FOSS case.
    2. Evidence-based medicine: systematic review methodology
       embedded as procedure [CONSORT, PRISMA guidelines as
       structure]. Prediction: fields that adopted procedural
       standards show less replication crisis than fields
       transmitting findings without procedural embedding.
       Status: partially testable against existing replication
       crisis literature by field.
    3. Agile software development [DISAMBIGUATED v18]:
       Two distinct populations exist; treating as single
       test case was the source of the contradiction with
       WIP 13 [PROPAGATION Mimicry candidate].
       POPULATION A -- Genuine L4 Propagation test:
         Organizations transmitting Agile as structure
         [retrospectives used to examine whether
         retrospectives work; sprint reviews used to
         test whether sprint review cadence is correct;
         meta-question present]. Tells: teams that
         have revised their own Agile process based
         on retrospective outputs. Methodology-as-
         method. Prediction: these organizations show
         no Generational Reset on team turnover.
       POPULATION B -- Propagation Mimicry candidate:
         Organizations transmitting Agile as ceremony
         [Scrum rituals performed without meta-question
         "is this ceremony producing correction?"].
         The procedure is the conclusion. Tell: teams
         where questioning the retrospective format is
         treated as "not doing Agile right." Methodology-
         as-conclusion. Prediction: Generational Reset
         on team turnover; new members receive ceremony,
         not revision capacity.
       MEASUREMENT INSTRUMENT [v18]:
         Ask: has the team ever revised its own Agile
         process based on retrospective outputs? If yes
         and revision stuck: Population A. If no, or
         revision was blocked as "not Agile": Population
         B. This is a behavioral test, not a survey --
         check git history for process changes initiated
         by retrospective outputs.
       RESOLUTION: Agile is not a single test. Population
         A tests L4 Propagation [methodology transmission].
         Population B tests Propagation Mimicry [ceremony-
         as-conclusion transmission]. Both predictions
         can be true simultaneously about different
         organizations. The contradiction in v17 was
         treating both populations as one test case.
         Status: both predictions open; measurement
         instrument defined; collect split-population
         data.
    Falsification: any of the three cases showing Generational
    Reset despite embedded methodology transmission, or
    sustained non-Reset despite conclusion-only transmission.

TOWER ORDER [primary organizing structure]

  FOUNDATION LAYER [enable all levels]:
  GENUINE AXIS             axis must exist before transmitting
  BOUNDARY INTEGRITY       defines who measurement applies to
