  TENSION DISSOLUTION      meta -- TENSION MAINTENANCE
                             opposite

  CONSTANTS [required from L1 upward]:
  CORRECTION CAPACITY      CONSTANT + L1 tension unit
  BOUNDARY INTEGRITY       CONSTANT
  TENSION MAINTENANCE      CONSTANT [meta]

  LEVEL 1 [dynamic -- generative]:
  GENERATIVE CYCLE         TENSION UNIT -- core
  SURPLUS PRODUCTION       TENSION UNIT
  CORRECTION CAPACITY      TENSION UNIT [also constant]
  Tests: T1.1-T1.5 [see tower block above]

  LEVEL 2 [dynamic -- epistemic]:
  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY    TENSION UNIT -- core
  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY        TENSION UNIT
  REFERENCE INTEGRITY      TENSION UNIT
  Tests: T2.1-T2.3 [see tower block above]

  LEVEL 3 [dynamic -- temporal]:
  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY TENSION UNIT -- core
  SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY       TENSION UNIT
  CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING    TENSION UNIT [also foundation]
  Tests: T3.1-T3.3 [see tower block above]

ANTI-COHERENCE TOWER ATTACK [probability gradient -- not
  necessary sequence]
  L3 attack: Fertile Lock -> corrupt transmission
  L2 attack: Measurement destruction -> surface captured
  L2 attack: Boundary manipulation -> measurement population
    shifted
  L2 attack: Causal orphaning -> chain severed
  L1 attack: Axis imposition -> direction captured
  L1 attack: Correction capture -> fires toward monopolist
  L1 attack: Consumption dominance -> cycle costs exceed
    output
  L1 attack: Cycle collapse -> generative loop fails
  Tower reading: attacks typically start at L3 [cheaper,
  less visible] and cascade downward. L1 attack is most
  catastrophic but most costly to execute. TRANSMISSION
  SELECTIVITY failure runs parallel not sequential. Each
  earlier step reduces resistance cost of later steps -- not
  a required sequence. Any step can fire first [Enron: L2
  without prior L3 failure; Room 101: L1 attacked directly].
  Read as: typical deployment order under resource
  constraints.

[HDR:VERSION_CHANGE_CONVENTION__V9_]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
VERSION CHANGE CONVENTION [v9]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

VERSION INCREMENT [vN -> vN+1] when:
  - New quality added to stack [quality stack level change]
  - New section added or sections restructured
  - Category renamed across full document [Structural
    Coherence rename]
  - New formal category added [Intrinsic, Nascent, Seeded
    Nascent]
  - New failure state added to measurement procedure
  - New honest limit category added [T2 Permanent]
  - New formal designation added [Evil/Good, Mixed Coherence]
  - New measurement track added [Track A-E]
  - Weapon index restructured or major weapon formally defined

[HDR:MERGE___REMOVE_LOGIC__EXECUTE_]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
MERGE / REMOVE LOGIC [execute at version change]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Run this checklist before finalizing any new version file.

REMOVE:
  - WIP items marked RESOLVED or CLOSED -- delete entirely
  - "deferred to vN" references where N <= current version
  - "Move to Pending" markers -- item should already be there
  - Duplicate section content [same definition in two
    sections]
  - Stale transcript-pending notes where content now confirmed
  - Orphan separator lines [=== or --- with no adjacent
    content]

MERGE:
  - Standalone findings that duplicate Theory section content
  - Psychology parallel + Fiction/Real table [same purpose]
  - Pending items that are fully complete -- remove from
    Pending
  - WIP sub-items that belong under a parent item

COMPRESS:
  - Version convention lists > 10 items -- summarize by
    category
  - Remove RESOLVED items from active list when next version
    is cut
  - Pending list items marked complete in all sub-points --
    remove

FORMAT:
  - Max 2 consecutive blank lines
  - No blank line immediately after === separator
  - No double === separators [run dedup pass]
  - No duplicate subsection headers within 10 lines of each
    other

AUDIT COMMANDS [run before saving]:
  Double separators:  count(===\n===) == 0
  Triple blanks:      count(\n\n\n) == 0
  Stale deferred:     count('deferred to v[N-1 or earlier]')
    == 0
  Orphan RESOLVED:    count('RESOLVED [v') in Active List
    == 0

QUALITY USAGE AUDIT [mandatory on version increment]:
  Run when: any quality added, removed, renumbered, or
  renamed.
  1. MAPPING TABLE: produce old->new quality name table
     before any changes. Archive in WIP Active List under
     version item.
  2. PROOF LABEL SCAN: grep all 'Proof:' and 'Proof for:'
     lines. Verify each quality name matches current schema.
     Dirty range = any Q that changed number or name.
  3. DEFINITION BLOCK SCAN: check internal cross-references
     within quality definitions [e.g. 'Distinct from
     MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY']. Update any reference into
     dirty range.
  4. WEAPON INDEX SCAN: verify each [HDR:QN_WEAPONS] tag
     matches the 'QN WEAPONS' content label directly below
     it.
  5. STRUCTURAL ORDER TABLE: update to match new
     assignments. This table is authoritative -- resolve
     conflicts against it.
  6. BODY TEXT: do NOT bulk-substitute in body text. Update
     only where meaning breaks. Quality names in context are
     robust; quality names in proof labels are not.
  7. CONFIRM: grep dirty-range quality names in proof labels
     after pass. Count must be zero before version is saved.
  If audit deferred: log as WIP item with mapping table
  inline. Undocumented renumber = document internally
  inconsistent.

FORMAT RULE: all lines <= 108 chars. Separators exempt.

SECTION AND MERGE LOGIC [applies everywhere]:
  AUTO-MERGE: two entries in same major section addressing
    same structural phenomenon from different angles ->
    merge on next version increment. Flag [MERGE CANDIDATE]
    if identified mid-session. Trigger: same quality target
    + same mechanism = merge. Trigger: sequential argument
    split across adjacent sections = merge. Trigger: one
    section is restatement of adjacent section = compress
    into cross-reference or subsection.

  ADD NEW SECTION: entry has distinct mechanism, distinct
    quality target, AND no existing section covers it within
    200 lines. Otherwise: add as subsection or extend nearest
    related entry.

  CROSS-REFERENCE instead of merge when: entries are in
    different section types [empirical vs speculative; method
    vs case library]. Boundary between sections is load-
    bearing -- do not collapse.

  COMPRESS: entry where full detail lives elsewhere ->
    reduce to core claim + pointer. Never duplicate
    substantial content.

  GIFT INDEX specific: Primary / Secondary / Closed /
    Sacrificial subcategories separated by ---------- for
    reading clarity. New entries slot into nearest existing
    subcategory.

  WEAPON INDEX specific: entries with identical quality
    target + mechanism -> merge with note. Sole proof of a
    type -> keep regardless of length. Remove only if no
    primary proof case.

NAME COMPRESSION POLICY:
  Abbreviations in use [used throughout for readability]:
    GA  = GENUINE AXIS
    BI  = BOUNDARY INTEGRITY
    SR  = SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
    TM  = TENSION MAINTENANCE
    TD  = TENSION DISSOLUTION
    GC  = GENERATIVE CYCLE
    SP  = SURPLUS PRODUCTION
    CC  = CORRECTION CAPACITY
    SD  = SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION
    MI  = MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
    CL  = CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
    RI  = REFERENCE INTEGRITY
    TS  = TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
    SA  = SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
    CAG = CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
    FC  = FORMATION CAPTURE
    MSC = MEASUREMENT SURFACE CONTROL
    ECC = EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION
    ICD = IDLE CYCLE DEPRIVATION
    ECD = ENGINEERED CAUSAL DIFFUSION
    CT  = CORRECTION THEATRE

WEAPON STACK DETECTION PROTOCOL [main theory -- v17,
  promoted from Section Two]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Promoted from Section Two: stacks are the primary
  deployment pattern in confirmed political and institutional
  cases. Single-weapon analysis misses load-bearing
  interaction effects. Stack detection changes both reading
  and intervention priority.

  5-STEP PROCEDURE:
    1. List all active weapons from case reading.
    2. Check for confirmed stack patterns [below].
    3. Identify interaction effects -- co-deployed weapons
       amplify each other's damage and reduce counter cost.
    4. Identify highest-ratio weapon in stack [cost-asymmetry
       table] -- this is intervention priority, not the most
       visible weapon.
    5. Confirm stack is load-bearing: remove one weapon from
       the model and check if the reading changes. If yes --
       it's a stack. If no -- coincidental co-deployment.

  CONFIRMED STACKS [interaction effects]:
    C2+C1 PRESSURE:
      MI degradation makes C1 invisible. System generates
      at full rate; surface cannot read depletion.
      Extraction appears healthy until substrate hits floor.
      Intervention on C2 alone triggers Diagnostic Collapse
      if C1 already terminal. Highest-risk stack: C2
      restoration is the standard intervention and it fires
      correctly into terminal C1. Pre-intervention: C1
      substrate audit mandatory. Do not open C2 on unaudited
      C1 when this stack is active.

    FC+ECC:
      Formation Capture installs axis; Exit Cost Construction
      makes revision structurally unavailable. FC alone =
      captured axis, revision possible at cost. ECC alone =
      high revision cost, axis still examinable. Stack =
      revision both psychologically blocked and structurally
      unavailable. Breaks the standard post-formation
      revision path [requires C1 starved + enabling moment
      + L3.5 matched contact -- ECC eliminates enabling
      moment by making exit too costly to reach substrate
      depletion].

    MSC+CT:
      Measurement Surface Control makes deviation unreadable;
      Correction Theatre makes invisible non-correction look
      like active correction. Stack = system appears to
      correct while surface is captured. External observer
      sees correction activity; no target changes. Standard
      tell: Correction Theatre firing at high volume
      alongside MSC = stack confirmed. Counter: measure
      outputs not correction events. If correction fires
      frequently but targets never change, MSC+CT stack is
      operating.

    ICD+FC:
      Idle Cycle Deprivation prevents axis formation;
      Formation Capture fills the gap with external content.
      ICD alone = axis doesn't form, gap present. FC alone
      = external content available, idle cycles required to
      install it. Stack = ICD creates the vacuum; FC fills
      it before internal generation can fire. No deployer
      coordination required -- structural co-deployment
      under attention architecture. Most common spontaneous
      stack in current population-scale conditions [social
      media + educational Formation Capture].

  INTERVENTION PRIORITY RULE:
    In a confirmed stack, always counter the highest-cost-
    asymmetry weapon first, not the most visible weapon.
    The most visible weapon is usually deployed to draw
    counter-attention away from the load-bearing weapon.
    Examples:
      C2+C1: C2 capture is visible; C1 depletion is not.
        Counter C1 depletion first; then restore C2.
      FC+ECC: ECC is visible [exit is blocked]; FC is not
        [axis capture is invisible from inside]. Reduce ECC
        to enable enabling moment; FC revision can then
        fire.
      MSC+CT: CT is visible; MSC is not [surface appears
        intact]. Identify independent MI surface before
        attacking CT -- CT without independent MI gives
        the extraction actor a new surface to capture.
      ICD+FC: ICD is structural [attention environment];
        FC is content [what fills the gap]. Restore idle
        cycles first; FC content loses delivery mechanism.

  CROSS-REFERENCE: case library political entries apply
    stack detection at Step 8 of Political Diagnostic
    Protocol. Stack findings feed back to intervention
    priority.

CASE STRENGTH RATING [v17 -- cross-library standard]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every case carries a composite strength rating [S1-S4]
  derived from three axes. Rating is independent of T3
  [analyst prior] -- T3 applies to political cases only.
  S-rating applies to all case types including fictional.

  AXIS 1 -- EVIDENCE TYPE [E]:
    E1: single fictional/illustrative source [structural
      illustration only; no independent confirmation
      possible by definition]
    E2: fictional + framework-consistent cross-reference
      [multiple fictional sources confirming same mechanism;
      still no real-world anchor]
    E3: real-world single domain [historical or empirical,
      one evidence type or one domain]
    E4: real-world multi-domain OR cross-domain independent
      confirmation [two or more independent evidence types
      or domains arriving at same finding without shared
      assumption]

  AXIS 2 -- MECHANISM CONFIRMATION [M]:
    M1: mechanism inferred from outcome only [no pathway
      described]
    M2: mechanism described + plausible pathway [internally
      consistent but no independent confirmation]
    M3: mechanism confirmed by independent parallel
      [psychology research / biological confirmation /
      historical analog in different domain]
    M4: mechanism confirmed by controlled comparison or
      near-control condition [same system, same conditions,
      one variable changed; or paired comparison with
      matched structure]

  AXIS 3 -- FALSIFIABILITY [F]:
    F1: no falsification criteria stated or structurally
      possible
    F2: falsification criteria stated, prediction made,
      not yet tested
    F3: falsification criteria stated, tested against one
      independent case, not falsified
    F4: falsification criteria stated, tested across
      multiple independent conditions in different domains,
      not falsified

  COMPOSITE [S-RATING]:
    S1 [ILLUSTRATION]: E1-2, any M, F1.
      Structural illustration -- valid for concept clarity
      and case presentation. Not confirmation. Do not cite
      as evidence for framework claims. Fictional cases
      are S1 unless cross-confirmed by E3+ finding.
    S2 [WEAK THEORY]: mixed scores with at least one axis
      at E3 or M3. Suggestive, not confirmed. One strong
      falsification would retire the finding to historical
      note. Use as directional indicator only.
    S3 [CONFIRMED]: E3+ AND M3+ AND F2+.
      Independent real-world evidence plus mechanism
      pathway plus stated falsification. Framework standard
      for a confirmed finding. Promote provisional findings
      to S3 when all three axes reached.
    S4 [STRONG]: E4 AND M4 AND F3+.
      Multi-domain independent confirmation plus controlled
      comparison plus tested falsification. Highest library
      confidence. Use as anchor for derived claims.

  FICTIONAL CASE CEILING:
    Fictional cases cap at S2 on E axis [E2 maximum].
    They cannot reach S3 without a real-world anchor
    confirming the same mechanism independently.
    Fictional cases are S1 [illustration] until a real-
    world parallel is found, then S2 [weak theory
    contribution to the cross-domain finding].
    A fictional case that is the sole proof of a framework
    claim = that claim is S1. Add E3+ evidence to promote.

  CURRENT HIGH-CONFIDENCE CASES [S4]:
    DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE: E4 [Glasnost + Libya/Tunisia
      controlled comparison, two independent real-world
      domains], M4 [mechanism confirmed by differential --
      same C2 opening, different C1 states, different
      outcomes], F3 [falsification tested against two cases
      + controlled comparison]. S4.
    L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION: E4 [Cistercian + Jesuit
      [two independent institutional domains] + Linux
      kernel [fast-cycle third domain]], M3 [mechanism
      confirmed by Protestant Reformation failure and Bell
      Labs partial failure], F3 [tested across three active
      cases, not falsified]. S4.

  S3 CONFIRMED FINDINGS [borderline S4]:
    C1 STARVED RECOVERY REQUIRES L3.5 CONTACT: E3 [five
      cases -- two real-world, three fictional; real-world
      anchor present], M3 [mechanism described + biological
      parallel confirmed], F2 [stated, no counterexample].
      S3. Promote to S4 when: independent institutional
      case with near-control condition confirmed.
    AXIS-MATCHING REQUIREMENT: E3 [five individual cases
      + China 1978 split], M4 [China 1978 split-axis
      differential = controlled comparison], F2 [stated,
      no falsification]. S3 borderline S4; promote when
      second split-axis institutional case confirmed.

  APPLICATION RULE:
    Assign E/M/F scores; derive S from composite. S-rating
    lives in case header alongside T3 where applicable.
    Provisional main theory findings require minimum S2 to
    remain in main theory. S1 entries move to Illustration
    Annex or annotated [ILLUSTRATION ONLY]. Any claim
    citing only S1 cases as support is flagged [STRUCTURAL
    READING -- EVIDENCE PENDING].

SECTION FIVE EPISTEMIC STATUS [v17 -- formal]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Section Five entries are structural readings. Falsification
  timelines are absent unless explicitly marked because
  political cases at civilizational scale have consequence
  horizons beyond testable prediction windows.

  DISTINCTION FROM VAGUE CAPTURED FIELD:
    VCF: theory unfalsifiable by design. Failures attributed
      to implementation to protect the frame. The frame
      itself is never tested.
    Section Five: mechanism claims are structural and real.
      Prediction timelines are absent because the horizon
      exceeds the window -- not because failure is pre-
      excused. The distinction is WHY prediction is absent,
      not whether it is absent.
    Test: if a Section Five claim were falsified, would the
      framework accept the falsification? Yes = structural
      reading with long horizon. No = VCF. Framework
      position: Section Five claims are falsifiable in
      principle; the window is too long for current
      tracking. Track where possible.

  REQUIREMENT [v17]:
    Any Section Five entry making a causal mechanism claim
    must carry either:
    [a] Explicit statement of why prediction window is
        structurally unavailable at current timescales.
    [b] A falsification condition even if timeline is long
        or open-ended.
    Entries without either: flagged as [STRUCTURAL READING
    -- PREDICTION PENDING]. This is not a demotion -- it
    is an honest limit marker. Framework standard.

  CURRENT STATUS [v18]:
    EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational]: carries falsification
      condition [v17 entry above]. Compliant.
    POWER ARCHITECTURE: falsification condition was present
      in epistemic block [v17]; header updated v18 to
      [FALSIFICATION CONDITION STATED]. Compliant.
    REALITY AS EXTRACTION CYCLE: explicit falsification
      condition added v18. Header updated to [FALSIFICATION
      CONDITION STATED]. Compliant.
    SOMATIC FORCING AS POLITICAL CORRECTION MECHANISM:
      carries three-condition falsifiable prediction with
      1-2 generation timeline [v17]. Compliant.

[WIP ITEM 13 -- ACTIVE]
L4 MIMICRY [candidate failure mode -- v17]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  WHY NEEDED:
    Coherence Mimicry confirmed at L1-L3 [HAL 9000 primary
    proof at L3; multiple weapon entries for L1-L2].
    L4 confirmation requires positive cases [three confirmed:
    Framework, Benedictine, Jesuit]. Without a named failure
    mode, confirmation criteria are asymmetric -- any apparent
    L4 is confirmatory; no case can disconfirm. Asymmetric
    confirmation = the same structure as Vague Captured Field.
    The framework cannot claim L4 is confirmed if it has no
    mechanism to detect false positives.

  CANDIDATE FAILURE MODES [not yet confirmed]:

    REFLEXIVITY Mimicry:
      System produces visible feedback pathway from correction
      outputs to correction architecture [appears reflexive]
      while feedback is routed to a captured surface. The
      architecture receives what it is supposed to receive --
      but the surface is wrong. Correction outputs are
      processed; the conclusions are captured before
      integration.
      Distinction from standard CC capture: standard capture
      is at object level. Reflexivity Mimicry is capture at
      meta-correction level. The system appears to correct
      its correction patterns while actually correcting
      toward monopolist benefit at meta level.
      Candidate case: academic reform cycles -- meta-reviews
      of peer review process appear reflexive [feedback
      pathway visible]; outputs consistently reproduce the
      captured surface [same journals, same metrics, same
      gatekeepers]. Stress event test: leadership change
      produces no change in captured surface. Possible
      Reflexivity Mimicry.
      Not yet confirmed: no direct T8 examination, no
      mechanism trace established.

    INTEGRATION Mimicry:
      System acknowledges correction pattern failure and
      incorporates language of revision into operating
      structure without changing operating structure.
      Distinct from Reflexive Oscillation [which identifies,
      fails to integrate, cycles back] -- Integration Mimicry
      integrates the language of the correction, not the
      correction itself. Terminology changes; mechanism
      stable.
      Tell: successive rounds of reform language that do not
      change measured outputs. Each generation of reform
      terminology absorbs the previous round's critique
      without changing the mechanism being critiqued.
      Candidate case: corporate DEI cycles -- each generation
      of diversity language absorbs the previous critique
      [we need diversity -> we need equity -> we need
      inclusion -> we need belonging] without changing
      measured representation or structural outcomes. Full
      language Integration; zero mechanism change.
      Not yet confirmed: mechanism trace required. Could be
      Integration Mimicry or genuine slow-change Integration
      with shorter-than-generation cycles.

    PROPAGATION Mimicry:
      System transmits apparent methodology [looks like T3.1
      high -- procedure is transmitted, not conclusion] that
      is actually a locked conclusion about methodology.
      The transmitted package is "how to run our correction
      procedure" as a conclusion rather than "how to examine
      and revise correction procedures" as a method.
      Tell: the transmitted procedure cannot be revised by
      successors without being told they are doing it wrong.
      Methodology-as-conclusion = Fertile Lock at meta level.
      Candidate case: Agile software methodology [Population
      B -- DISAMBIGUATED v18]. Scrum ceremonies transmitted
      as locked procedure in organizations where questioning
      the ceremony is "not doing Agile right." Meta-question
      absent. The ceremony is the conclusion; revision
      capacity not transmitted. This is specifically
      Population B [see L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION -- Active
      Test Cases for split definition].
      Disambiguation: Population A [Agile orgs that have
      revised their own process based on retrospective
      outputs] is the L4 PROPAGATION confirmation candidate.
      Population B is the Propagation Mimicry candidate.
      These are separate predictions about different orgs,
      not contradictory predictions about the same test.
      Not yet confirmed: requires collecting Population B
      outcome data [Generational Reset on team turnover,
      ceremony preserved unchanged across generations].
      Measurement instrument: defined in L4 PROPAGATION
      PREDICTION entry -- behavioral test, not survey.

  IMPOSSIBILITY ARGUMENT [track -- do not close without
    examining]:
    Argument for impossibility: genuine REFLEXIVITY cannot
    be mimicked because it requires actual feedback to
    correction architecture, not surface performance of
    feedback. At L1-L3, captured surfaces can maintain
    appearance. At L4, the feedback goes to the architecture
    itself -- you cannot fake receiving feedback you are
    not receiving without the fake becoming indistinguishable
    from the real at all levels. If L4 Mimicry is
    structurally impossible, the confirmation asymmetry
    problem is resolved differently: L4 is self-confirming
    by structural necessity once the three tension units
    are operational.
    Counterargument: INTEGRATION Mimicry does not require
    faking feedback reception. It requires that the
    integration step captures the correction output before
    it changes the architecture. The feedback is received;
    the integration is captured. This may be possible
    without the impossibility argument applying.
    Resolution: close as impossible only if argument
    eliminates INTEGRATION Mimicry specifically. Currently
    open.

  CONFIRMATION REQUIREMENT:
    L4 Mimicry confirmed when: documented case where all
    three tension units appear functional at T8 examination
    but stress event [leadership change, institutional
    crisis, successor-generation review] reveals meta-
    correction architecture was captured, oscillating, or
    transmitting procedure-conclusions rather than procedure-
    methods.
    Candidate stress-event test: any institution claiming L4
    record with 2+ generations -- does Generational Reset
    occur on leadership change? If yes: Mimicry candidate.
    If no: confirmation strengthens.
    No confirmed case in current library.

  FALSIFICATION OF THIS WIP ENTRY:
    L4 Mimicry does not exist as a failure mode if the
    impossibility argument holds for all three candidate
    subtypes. Close WIP 13 if: [1] impossibility argument
    extended to cover INTEGRATION Mimicry, AND [2] no
    candidate case survives T8 examination with confirmed
    Mimicry mechanism. Keep open if either condition unmet.

L4.5 CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [WIP Item 14 -- v18]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Hypothesized state between confirmed institutional L4
  [Cistercian, Jesuit] and L5 [undefined].

  OPERATIONAL DEFINITION:
    L4 meta-correction running at civilizational scale
    with method [not just conclusions] crossing the
    generational transmission boundary intact. Distinct
    from institutional L4 [contained, non-civilizational]
    and from L5 [unknown content, requires L4.5 as
    precondition].

  STATUS: No confirmed positive case in library.
    S3 PATTERN EVIDENCE [v18]: three independent near-cases
    with consistent termination mechanism. Not a confirmed
    case -- termination mechanism is consistent but
    absence of positive case means causal direction is
    not confirmed. Pattern is S3 [E3: three real-world
    independent near-cases; M3: mechanism described +
    confirmed across cases + consistent with ANTI-COHERENCE
    COST ASYMMETRY logic; F2: falsification condition
    stated]. Promote to S4 when positive case confirmed.

  THREE NEAR-CASES [all terminated before transmission
  completed]:
    Athens [~450-380 BCE]:
      Socratic method as institutionalized meta-
      correction. Procedure [dialectic] transmitted not
      conclusion. Cross-domain outputs [Plato, Aristotle,
      geometry, logic] only possible with L4 running.
      Termination: execution of Socrates [399 BCE] =
      correction capacity visible enough to threaten
      political architecture. Destroyed before
      transmission window closed.
    Islamic Golden Age [~800-1100 CE]:
      Translation movement + ijtihad [independent
      reasoning] as institutionalized SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
      at civilizational scale. Cross-domain synthesis
      [algebra, optics, medicine, philosophy] confirmed.
      Termination: Ash'ari theological capture of ijtihad
      [closing of independent reasoning ~1100 CE].
      Correction monopoly constructed at theological
      scale. Diagnostic Collapse candidate. Recovery
      partial, never restored.
    Scottish Enlightenment [~1740-1790]:
      High-density L4-adjacent node [Hume, Smith, Hutton,
      Black, Watt] in genuine cross-domain intellectual
      friction. Outputs [political economy, geology,
      chemistry, steam engineering] only possible with
      meta-correction running cross-domain.
      Termination: outputs extracted, method abandoned.
      Protestant Reformation pattern at Enlightenment
      scale -- conclusions crossed transmission boundary,
      procedure did not. TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY failure.

  CONSISTENT TERMINATION MECHANISM [across all three]:
    Correction capacity became visible enough to threaten
    extraction architecture. Architecture absorbed or
    destroyed it before transmission closed. Political
    or theological capture in every case.

  NEAR-CASE STRESS-TEST [v18 -- alternative explanations]:
    ALT 1 [Athens]: Socrates killed for personal enemies
      and impiety charge, not systemic correction threat.
      Against: the impiety charge is the mechanism, not
      an alternative. Correction capacity at civilizational
      visibility = institutional threat. The legal vehicle
      is extraction architecture using available instrument.
      That the charge was formally religious does not
      change the structural function. Plato's subsequent
      withdrawal [Republic as political theory, not
      engaged practice] and Aristotle's caution confirm
      the chilling effect on meta-correction as method.
      Termination mechanism holds.
    ALT 2 [Islamic Golden Age]: decline was Mongol
      invasion [1258 Baghdad sack], not theological
      capture.
      Against: Ash'ari capture of ijtihad is documented
      ~1100 CE -- 158 years before the Mongol sack.
      The correction methodology was already closed
      before the military catastrophe. Mongol invasion
      accelerated substrate collapse but was not the
      termination mechanism for L4.5. Two separate
      events; framework claim is about the theological
      capture of ijtihad, not the military collapse.
      Termination mechanism holds independently.
    ALT 3 [Scottish Enlightenment]: outputs were
      transmitted [political economy, geology] -- this
      contradicts TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY failure.
      Against: this is the TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
      finding, not a contradiction of it. Conclusions
      transmitted; method did not. Adam Smith's market
      conclusions were transmitted as doctrine; his
      method [empirical observation of labor conditions,
      outputs as truth test] was not transmitted as
      structure. Ricardo and subsequent economists
      transmitted Smith's conclusions as locked frame.
      The outputs crossed the boundary; the procedure
      that generated them did not. This is the
      Protestant Reformation pattern at intellectual
      scale. Termination mechanism holds.
    Stress-test result: all three alternative explanations
      examined. None overturn the consistent termination
      mechanism. S3 pattern evidence confirmed v18.

  INTERNET-ERA POSITIVE CANDIDATE [v18 -- open]:
    Anti-capture architecture requirement [per PREDICTION
    below] raises the question whether distributed
    digital infrastructure constitutes an anti-capture
    instrument. Candidate: open-source / open-access
    ecosystem as distributed correction architecture.
    Wikipedia + FOSS + arXiv + peer-to-peer protocols
    collectively distribute correction capacity across
    nodes that no single political or theological actor
    can monopolize.
    Against: platform capture [GitHub/Microsoft, Reddit
    API closures, Twitter/X centralization] shows
    the architecture is not immune to capture. Each
    node individually capturable; the question is
    whether all nodes simultaneously capturable.
    Current status: not confirmed. Requires: evidence
    of meta-correction [not just information] running
    at civilizational scale with method [not just
    conclusion] transmitting across generations.
    Wikipedia has one generational cycle [~20 years].
    Early evidence: methodology [NPOV + talk page
    dispute resolution] shows some structural
    transmission. Counter-evidence: culture wars on
    Wikipedia suggest method is under sustained
    capture pressure. Track.
    Note: this is the first internet-era candidate
    in the library. Warrants a dedicated case entry
    once one full generational cycle is documentable.

  PREDICTION [falsifiable]:
    L4.5 requires anti-capture architecture as
    precondition. Correction instrument must be
    distributed enough that no single political or
    theological node can monopolize it.
    Current candidate: none confirmed.
    Falsification condition: confirmed case of
    civilizational L4 transmission completing without
    capture termination. If found, upgrade to confirmed
    finding and open L5 prediction window.

  L5 NOTE:
    Content of L5 unpredictable from current evidence.
    Structural signature probably: something that only
    becomes possible when meta-correction is the default
    operating mode of a civilization rather than the
    exception produced by rare high-density nodes under
    favorable conditions. MORAL LEGIBILITY operating at
    civilizational scale [ethics-as-empirical-instrument
    as default not exception] is one candidate property.
    Cannot confirm without L4.5 completing first.
    Honest limit declared. Do not speculate beyond this.

  Cross-ref: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [confirmed
    v18 -- generation only has to win once; cost
    asymmetry logic predicts why capture always fires
    before transmission closes]; TRANSMISSION
    SELECTIVITY [method vs conclusion distinction
    load-bearing]; PROTESTANT REFORMATION [conclusions

SECTION FIVE -- POLITICAL DIAGNOSTIC PROTOCOL
[SEC:POLITICAL_DIAGNOSTIC_PROTOCOL]
==========================================================================
Self-contained reading instrument. Applies nested cycle model +
trajectory model to political systems. Can be used without full
framework apparatus.

PRE-READING REQUIREMENTS:
  1. BOUNDARY DECLARATION: name Boundary A [unit being measured],
     Boundary B [population bearing costs], meta-boundary [system
     containing both].
  2. ANALYST PRIOR: state position before reading. Framework derives
     from Mutual Ordoliberal Distributist position [v15].
     Known blind spots: preference for distributed ownership may
     underweight efficiency arguments for scale; subsidiarity
     assumption may miss coordination problems requiring
     centralization; anti-extraction frame may over-identify
     extraction in systems with genuine mixed function.
  3. INSTRUMENT PROFILE: who is measuring, what instrument, what
     position, what blind spots. AI: declare training constraints
     and safety fine-tuning asymmetries. Human: declare national,
     cultural, professional position.

READING PROCEDURE [12 steps]:
  1.  BOUNDARY TEST: all three boundaries named and distinct?
  2.  CYCLE STATUS: C1/C2/C3 for Boundary A.
  3.  TRAJECTORY: direction and rate.
  4.  COUNTERFACTUAL: what would system look like if primary stated
      function were genuine? Gap from actual?
  5.  CORRECTION CAPACITY TEST: distributed or monopolized?
      Aimed at actual cause or proxy? [T2.2 A+B both required]
  6.  WINDOW SPLIT: expand and contract window -- reading change?
      If yes, window is load-bearing; name correct window.
  7.  ANALYST PRIOR TEST: reading change if prior reversed?
      If yes, flag and state both readings.
  8.  ADVERSARIAL READING: invert conclusion, not moderate it.
      Strongest case that reading is wrong. Does it survive?
  9.  THIRD POPULATION TEST: who is not in A or B?
      What does system look like from their position?
  10. WEAPON BENEFICIARY CHECK: who benefits from active weapon?
      Follow the surplus.
  11. PREDICTION: one falsifiable claim with timeline. Specify
      what falsifies it.
  12. EXTERNAL ACTOR DISCLOSURE: name all external actors with
      active interests. Failure to name = T3 High on meta-boundary.

TRAJECTORY INDICATORS:
  Toward coherence: correction pathways activating against high-power
    deviators; C2 instruments diversifying; weapon deployment
    decreasing or becoming more visible; exit costs declining.
  Holding: correction fires against low-power targets only; C2
    stable but not expanding; weapon deployment steady.
  Away: correction pathway conditions 2 and 4 weakening; C2 capture
    accelerating; exit costs rising; external actor influence growing.

CENTRALIZATION SCORING RUBRIC [four-condition test]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Condition 1: Does at least one correction pathway exist that can
    fire against the central node?
  Condition 2: Is that pathway structurally independent of the
    central node?
  Condition 3: Has the pathway demonstrated activation under stress
    [not just paper existence]?
  Condition 4: Does activation not require the central node's
    permission?

  Scoring: 4/4 = coherent centralization. 3/4 = marginal.
    2/4 or below = anti-coherent regardless of stated intent.

  WORKED EXAMPLE -- US Federal Government:
    C1: impeachment, elections, judicial review. PASSES.
    C2: Congress owns impeachment but parties not independent
      of executive; judiciary appointments create dependency.
      CONTESTED [~0.5].
    C3: Nixon [resigned before completion], Clinton impeachment
      initiated. Activation demonstrated. PASSES.
    C4: passes in theory; party loyalty makes it functionally
      compromised in practice. PARTIAL [~0.5].
    Score: 2.5-3/4. Mixed. Degrading over time as C2 and C4
    weaken through party capture of congressional correction
    capacity.

  WORKED EXAMPLE -- Imperium of Man [40K]:
    C1: No pathway to correct Emperor or High Lords. FAILS.
    Score: 0/4. Anti-coherent by first condition.

POLICY POSITIONS:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

    10-15 years, not lifetime. Recall or panel review for

    [L4] -- feedback pathway from correction outputs to

  Immigration [personal]: H1B-equivalent with minimum $100,000
    salary floor, mandatory return, no foreign bank transfers

    only because public infrastructure absorbs the differential.

    obligation by default. No automatic mother preference.

    rapid retry option. Simple fast methods only. Overreaction

  Welfare: through infrastructure and direct provision [housing,
    healthcare, food, transport] not cash. Backup not

    GDP = no recession. Purchasing power, wage growth vs

REALITY AS EXTRACTION CYCLE [STRUCTURAL READING -- FALSIFICATION CONDITION STATED]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Nations and corporations are extraction nodes in a self-
  maintaining cycle. Accumulate surplus by externalizing substrate
  costs onto populations that bear the death and hunger. Actors
  who could correct it benefit from its continuation -- correction
  mechanism owned by the corrected actor [four-condition test
  fails at every scale]. The death and hunger are not side effects
  -- they are the substrate cost being externalized. This is the
  operating system, not a malfunction.
  [Epistemic status: structural reading. Causal mechanism confirmed
  across historical record. Prediction window structurally
  unavailable at current timescales -- 10,000-year pattern; any
  single prediction would require civilizational-scale window.
  Cross-ref: EXTRACTION CYCLE [main theory v17] for oscillation
  pattern and declining substrate prediction with falsification
  condition.]
  [Falsification condition [v18 -- added to clear PREDICTION
  PENDING status]:
    Finding is falsified if: documented multi-generational
    [3+ generations] power structure exists at civilizational
    scale [continent or larger, 50M+ population] where:
    [a] surplus flows to majority by structural design not
        coincidence [i.e., the mechanism routes surplus
        outward rather than capturing it upward], AND
    [b] correction mechanism is not owned by the corrected
        actor [four-condition test passes at the top level],
        AND
    [c] substrate costs are borne proportionally, not
        externalized downward.
    No confirmed case in library. This is the falsification
    condition, not a prediction window -- the claim is that
    this configuration has not occurred, not that it cannot
    occur. If found: retire to historical note and open
    investigation of what structural conditions enabled it.]

NATURAL STATE AS THREAT TO NATIONS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Natural state [local community, subsistence, direct exchange,
  somatic coherence, genuine CC] produces people who don't need
  the legitimacy surface, don't depend on state infrastructure,
  and can read extraction accurately. Nation-states require
  dependency to function. Natural state produces independence.
  Suppression of natural state = prerequisite for extraction
  architecture. Enclosure, urbanization, credential gatekeeping,
  monetary dependency -- all convert natural state independence
  into state dependency. Not incidental side effects.
  T9 application: admitting the natural state was suppressed
  deliberately collapses the civic identity built around
  progress and civilization.

PEOPLE AS SUBSTRATE AND AGENT [framework-derived]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Both simultaneously. Formation conditions shape substrate.
  Individuals choose within those conditions.
  Origin is structural; perpetuation requires individual choice.
  Attribution error in either direction is RI capture:
    Attributing structural formation failure to individual
    moral deficiency = calibration shifts to character judgment.
    Treating structural origin as permanent determinism =
    removes individual agency entirely.
  Framework position: both are true. Track both.

  Direct hierarchy [legible]: explicit chain of command,
  visible authority, correctable by targeting the node.

  declared source. No single target for correction.

  correction address. Diffuse captures through a thousand
  small compliance events with no single responsible actor.
  Stealing not predation at institutional scale.

FORCE-REVEAL / COSTLY REMOVAL [survival under pressure]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  True silence may be impossible at sufficient scale. Two
  destruction pressures: evil must destroy [extraction
  architecture threatened]; not-evil must destroy [outside
  measurement surface reads as illegitimate by REFERENCE
  INTEGRITY capture]. Both produce identical pressure.
  Costly removal as viable alternative: raise cost of four-
  condition test against yourself -- make conditions 2 and 4
  fail for the attacker. Not invisible; too expensive to
  remove cleanly.
  Operationally -- raise removal cost by:
    Distributed structure [no single point of failure].
    Output legibility [outputs speak before structure is
      legible enough to target].
    Legal / institutional embedding [requires formal process
      to remove].
    Redundancy [multiple actors can perform the function].
  Test: would removing this structure cost more than it
  produces in extraction gain for the attacker? If yes,
  removal is structurally irrational.

SOMATIC FORCING AS POLITICAL CORRECTION MECHANISM [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STRUCTURAL READING -- PREDICTION PRESENT. Falsifiable
  prediction with 1-2 generation timeline below. Compliant
  with Section Five Epistemic Status requirement.]

BOUNDARY A: populations under increasing physical substrate
  pressure [cost of living, food security, environmental
  degradation, health].
BOUNDARY B: extraction architecture beneficiaries.
META-BOUNDARY: civilizational system containing both.
Cross-ref: EXTRACTION CYCLE [main theory v17] -- somatic
  forcing is the mechanism by which each cycle fires.

CORE CLAIM:
  Reasoning-layer correction can be captured [axis
  substitution, calibration strip, motivated cognition all
  operate at deliberative level]. Somatic response fires
  before reasoning layer can intercept. When substrate
  pressure becomes physically felt rather than institutionally
  mediated, correction fires at somatic level regardless of
  captured ideology.
  Body registers threat without requiring ideological
  interpretation. Hunger, physical insecurity, environmental
  collapse don't need media to frame them. The instrument is
  atrophied at reasoning level; somatic response is harder
  to capture and harder to fake.

MECHANISM:
  ICD applied to cognition produces atrophied reasoning
  instrument in populations -- undeveloped capacity, not
  stupidity. Somatic forcing bypasses this. Physical substrate
  pressure activates correction capacity that ideology cannot
  suppress because it operates below the capture layer.
  This is why extraction architectures work hard to keep
  substrate pressure just below somatic forcing threshold --
  enough deprivation to maintain dependency, not enough to
  trigger somatic correction that bypasses reasoning capture.

WEAPONS ACTIVE [preventing somatic forcing from becoming
  correction]:
  Horizon Extension: extend timeline of deterioration past
    correction window. Boil the frog.
  Proxy Construction: route somatic distress toward decoy
    targets. Somatic signal real; correction aimed
    incorrectly.
  Intermittent Reinforcement: periodic substrate relief
    prevents somatic forcing from accumulating past
    threshold.
  Exit Cost Construction: somatic forcing fires but exit
    costs prevent action. Body knows; behavior constrained
    by structural dependency.

TIMING PROBLEM [Diagnostic Collapse risk]:
  Somatic forcing arrives at one of two times:
    Early enough [C1 recoverable]: correction fires,
      substrate can absorb it. Recovery possible.
      Vatican II pattern.
    Too late [C1 terminal]: correction fires correctly
      but substrate cannot absorb the correction load.
      Diagnostic Collapse. Glasnost pattern.
  Extraction architecture's primary goal: ensure somatic
  forcing arrives in the second window not the first.
  Horizon Extension + substrate management aimed at this
  timing specifically. History: approximately even split.
  No structural guarantee.

TRAJECTORY [current]:
  C1 substrate [material]: degrading in most high-extraction
    populations. Wealth concentration, housing, food security
    all moving in extraction direction.
  C2 [epistemic]: heavily captured. Proxy Construction
    consuming correction attention. Reasoning layer
    weaponized.
  Somatic pressure: accumulating. Below forcing threshold in
    most populations currently. Horizon Extension active.
  M1: -1 [away]. M2: SLOW. M3: SELF-REINFORCING [extraction
    produces conditions for more extraction before forcing
    fires].

PETRODOLLAR PARALLEL:
  Dollar-denominated oil pricing = MSC + Calibration Strip
  at civilizational scale. When energy costs produce somatic
  forcing [cold, hunger, economic collapse], correction fires
  against the visible mechanism [fuel prices, government
  policy] not the underlying capture architecture [reserve
  currency hegemony]. Proxy Construction operates precisely
  here -- somatic signal real, correction aimed at proxy,
  capture architecture protected.

PREDICTION [falsifiable]:
  Somatic forcing produces political correction when three
  conditions met simultaneously:
    1. Physical substrate pressure crosses threshold
       [cannot be dismissed as ideological framing].
    2. Proxy Construction fails [decoy target clearly
       insufficient to explain substrate pressure].
    3. Exit costs low enough that correction can fire
       into action, not just awareness.
  When all three present: rapid non-linear correction.
  Current status: Condition 1 accumulating. Condition 2
  holding [proxies still absorbing correction attention].
  Condition 3 high [exit costs elevated deliberately].
  Prediction: Condition 2 fails before Condition 1 becomes
  terminal. Proxy exhaustion precedes substrate collapse
  in high-information environments.
  Timeline: 1-2 generations.
  Falsification: Condition 1 reaches terminal before
  Condition 2 fails. Diagnostic Collapse. Substrate
  depleted before correction fires effectively.

REVOLUTIONARY VIOLENCE -- STRUCTURAL PATTERN [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
EPISTEMIC NOTE: structural reading only. Judgment about
whether violence is justified under specific conditions is
the person's determination, not the framework's.

ANALYST PRIOR [declared]:
  Violence is a very effective solution that removes all
  other solutions. Most people who start revolutions either
  don't understand this, don't want to, or have replacing
  one extraction architecture with another as the actual
  goal. Adversarial reading: some revolutions had no
  available alternative path [Haitian Revolution, colonial
  independence under active oppression]. The prior holds
  as a structural observation about internal revolution
  specifically.

TWO STRUCTURALLY DISTINCT CASE TYPES:

  COLONIAL INDEPENDENCE [external extraction removed]:
    External extraction architecture imposed by outside
    power. Revolution removes the external layer.
    Pre-existing local correction capacity can be restored.
    Cases: American Revolution [colonial assemblies pre-
    existed], Haitian Revolution [ended imposed chattel
    slavery], Indian independence [partially nonviolent --
    confirms external extraction can be removed without
    full internal revolution].
    Structural outcome: often improved. External extraction
    removed; local correction capacity restored or rebuilt.

  INTERNAL REVOLUTION [existing government overthrown]:
    Destroys existing correction infrastructure. Must build
    replacement using the centralized methodology required
    to win -- hierarchical coordination, suppressed internal
    dissent, concentrated force.
    Structural problem: methodology required to win is
    incompatible with stated goal. Centralized methodology
    produces centralized outcome regardless of intent.
    Cannot transmit distributed correction goal through
    centralized revolutionary method.
    Cases: French Revolution -> Napoleon; Russian Revolution
    -> Stalin; Chinese Revolution -> Mao.
    Structural outcome: consistent reproduction of extraction
    architecture with different actors. Not coincidence --
    structural consequence of methodology mismatch.

  AMERICAN REVOLUTION [partial exception, explains why]:
    Had unusual features that partially broke the pattern:
    existing distributed governance infrastructure [colonial
    assemblies], legal/philosophical framework transmitted
    beforehand [Federalist Papers = methodology not just
    conclusions], federated structure built in from start.
    Closer to colonial independence than internal revolution
    structurally.

  T3: case attribution contested. Structural pattern confirmed
    across multiple cases; individual attribution to
    revolution as cause vs other factors partially obscured.

CAPTURE-RESISTANT REGULATION [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
EPISTEMIC NOTE: analyst prior [Mutual Ordoliberal Distributist]
favors distributed correction capacity and skepticism toward
discretionary authority.

STRUCTURAL OBSERVATION:
  Hyper-regulation of legible technical activities coexists
  with light regulation of diffuse financial and political
  extraction. Pattern explained by capture surface:
    Technical activities: clear actors, measurable outputs,
      concentrated opponents = easy to regulate AND
      over-regulate with compliance theater.
    Financial extraction: diffuse beneficiaries, captured
      measurement surfaces, actors embedded in regulatory
      apparatus = structurally resistant to regulation.

REGULATION TYPES RESISTANT TO CAPTURE:
  Outcome-based [not process-based]: measures what happens,
    not whether procedures were followed.
  Bright-line rules [not discretionary judgment]: speed
    limits, blood alcohol levels, structural load ratings.
  Self-enforcing via liability: harmed parties sue directly.
    Tort law more capture-resistant than agency rulemaking.
  Distributed enforcement: multiple independent actors
    [state AGs + federal + private right of action].
  Transparency requirements: mandatory disclosure not
    agency approval.

COMMON THREAD: capture-resistant regulation reduces
  discretion from human-to-human negotiation and routes
  through mechanisms harder to compromise.

ADVERSARIAL READING:
  Bright lines can be gamed at the boundary [Goodhart's
  Law]. Self-enforcement advantages wealthy plaintiffs.
  Transparency without comprehension produces information
  overload not accountability. Each mechanism has a failure
  mode; the question is whether the failure mode is harder
  to exploit than agency capture. Evidence suggests yes for
  most contexts.

T3: empirical claims about which regulation types resist
  capture are contested. Framework structural analysis
  consistent; implementation evidence mixed.

SETI FUNDING AS POLITICAL INSTRUMENT [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
BOUNDARY A: international SETI/astrobiology funding apparatus.
BOUNDARY B: global population bearing cost of non-discovery.
META-BOUNDARY: global institutional extraction architecture
  whose legitimacy narratives depend on current information
  environment.

CYCLE STATUS:
  C1 [generative]: genuine scientific capacity present.
    C1 functional at researcher level.
  C2 [epistemic]: captured at funding-allocation level. The
    most sensitive searches [thermodynamic anomaly at stellar
    scale, structured geometric transit analysis] are
    systematically unfunded. C2 reads "what looks legitimate
    to funding committees" not "what would actually find
    something."
  C3 [temporal]: Fertile Lock candidate. Same methods
    transmitted generation to generation not because most
    likely to succeed but because what got funded before.

WEAPONS ACTIVE:
  Calibration Strip: "legitimate science" defined as radio/
    biosignature by institutional precedent. Megastructure
    search carries stigma from mid-20th century UFO
    contamination.
  Correction Theatre: funding bodies perform search via
    budget line items without allocating enough to actually
    complete discriminating survey.
  Proxy Construction: UFO discourse as decoy. Correction
    budget consumed by proxy; actual search underfunded.

BENEFICIARY OF CURRENT PATTERN [follow the surplus]:
  Structural stack, diffuse beneficiary. Entities whose
  control architecture depends on uniqueness narratives,
  information scarcity, and political legitimacy all benefit
  from non-discovery. No coordination required.

ANTARCTICA PARALLEL:
  Antarctica: stable equilibrium -- all parties benefit from
    current non-exploitation status.
  SETI: all institutional actors benefit from non-discovery.
    Search funded at legitimacy threshold, not discovery
    threshold. Antarctica in space.

CENTRALIZATION SCORE: 1/4. No mechanism to force funding
  reallocation to discriminating methods.

PREDICTION [falsifiable]:
  If $500M were allocated to dedicated thermodynamic anomaly
  survey + structured transit analysis across full Milky Way
  stellar sample, either a confirmed technosignature would
  be found or the search space would be genuinely exhausted.
  Current trajectory: neither occurs. Budget stays at
  legitimacy threshold. Search space coverage in 2050:
  still less than 0.1% of Milky Way.
  Falsification: sustained $500M+/year funding for
  discriminating technosignature search over 10-year window.

MAINTENANCE LOGIC:
  MERGE: identical quality target + mechanism -> merge with note.
  REMOVE: weapon candidates with no primary proof case.
  COMPRESS: entries > 15 lines without unique proof -> trim to
    name/type/proof-for/weapons-active.
  KEEP: sole proof of quality/weapon/failure state.
  SPACE TRIGGERS: >400KB compress. >500KB retire secondaries.
    >300 cases -> dedup before adding.
  SESSION START: remove RESOLVED/CLOSED items, renumber from 1.

WEAPON COUNTER-MEASUREMENTS:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every weapon targets a specific quality; restoring or protecting
  that quality counters it.
  Direct counters:
    MSC -> independent measurement instrument not owned by attacker.
    Calibration Strip -> multiple competing reference standards.
    Formation Capture -> delayed axis formation; external exposure
      before lock-in.
    Exit Cost Construction -> reduce dependency, build alternative
      substrate.
    Correction Theatre -> require output verification not activity.
  Hard case: weapons targeting MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY first.
    Cannot verify counter-effectiveness using captured instrument.
    Counter requires external instrument not owned by attacker.
  Impossible case: Formation Capture at earliest developmental
    window. Counter capacity not yet formed. Counter must come
    from outside the affected system entirely.

WEAPON COST-ASYMMETRY TABLE [v16 -- strategic]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Every weapon has three cost dimensions:
  DEPLOY: cost to deployer to activate and sustain the weapon.
  DAMAGE: quality degradation imposed on target per unit time.
  COUNTER: cost to target to neutralize or resist.
Scale: LOW / MED / HIGH / EXTREME. Ratio = DAMAGE / DEPLOY.
High ratio = efficient weapon [cheap to deploy, expensive to counter].

HIGHEST RATIO WEAPONS [DAMAGE >> DEPLOY -- priority targets]:

  Idle Cycle Deprivation
    Deploy: LOW [side-effect of engagement optimization; no
      additional investment required beyond existing attention
      economy infrastructure]
    Damage: HIGH [SR deficit accumulates; self-reinforcing loop;
      C1 substrate degrades continuously without visible signal]
    Counter: HIGH [requires environmental redesign; individual
      CC depleted before it can fire against weapon; structural
      intervention only]
    Ratio: HIGH/LOW = extreme asymmetry. Most efficient weapon
      in index by cost ratio.

  Formation Capture [AGC]
    Deploy: LOW-MED [requires access to formation window; axis
      installs via normal relational contact -- no special
      mechanism required beyond presence and repetition]
    Damage: EXTREME [axis capture is total; C2 and C3 transmit
      the capture forward; generational propagation at zero
      marginal cost]
    Counter: EXTREME [counter capacity not yet formed at window;
      external intervention only; window closes; T7 applies]
    Ratio: EXTREME/LOW = highest damage-to-deploy ratio in index.
      Window-dependent -- outside the window, weapon unavailable.

  Calibration Strip
    Deploy: LOW [requires only reference standard insertion at
      calibration point; single intervention propagates forward
      through all downstream measurements automatically]
    Damage: HIGH [all measurements inherit miscalibration; CAUSAL
      LEGIBILITY orphaned; C2 runs honestly on wrong baseline;
      produces confident false readings rather than detectable
      errors]
    Counter: HIGH [requires independent external reference standard
      that deployer does not control; hard to obtain once strip
      is established as the consensus baseline]
    Ratio: HIGH/LOW = extreme.

  Engineered Causal Diffusion [ECD]
    Deploy: MED [requires structuring information environment to
      route all causal chains through complexity nodes; needs
      institutional access but not operational maintenance]
    Damage: HIGH [CAUSAL LEGIBILITY destroyed; correction cannot
      anchor; C1 continues generating without traceable feedback]
    Counter: HIGH [requires independent causal reconstruction from
      outside the diffusion architecture]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.

  Correction Theatre
    Deploy: LOW [substitute visible correction activity for actual
      correction; requires only institutional control of correction
      surface]
    Damage: MED-HIGH [CORRECTION CAPACITY budget consumed on
      Theatre; actual deviations accumulate uncorrected]
    Counter: MED [require output verification not activity;
      detectable if external measurement surface available]
    Ratio: MED-HIGH/LOW = efficient.

MED RATIO WEAPONS [DAMAGE ~ DEPLOY]:

  Measurement Surface Control [MSC]
    Deploy: HIGH [requires capturing or constructing the primary
      measurement surface; significant investment in institutional
      access, credentialing, or platform control]
    Damage: EXTREME [once surface controlled, all readings route
      through captured instrument; no independent verification
      available from inside boundary]
    Counter: HIGH [independent external instrument; expensive
      to build and maintain against resource asymmetry]
    Ratio: EXTREME/HIGH = efficient despite high deploy cost.
    Note: highest absolute damage in index.

  Exit Cost Construction [ECC]
    Deploy: MED [requires substrate dependency construction over
      time; housing, income, identity, community]
    Damage: HIGH [exit = total substrate loss; GENUINE AXIS
      cannot fire independently; T1.4 low]
    Counter: MED-HIGH [requires alternative substrate construction
      before exit]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.

  Proxy Construction
    Deploy: MED [requires manufacturing and maintaining the decoy
      target; needs media or institutional access]
    Damage: HIGH [CORRECTION CAPACITY budget consumed on proxy;
      actual extraction architecture protected]
    Counter: MED [name the proxy and trace actual causal chain]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.

  Rhythm Disruption
    Deploy: LOW-MED [precarity architecture; variable scheduling]
    Damage: MED-HIGH [CYCLE STABILITY destroyed; C1 cannot form
      reliable cycle; stack with ICD for maximum C1 pressure]
    Counter: MED [stable scheduling; protected cycle windows;
      requires structural not individual intervention]
    Ratio: MED-HIGH/LOW-MED = efficient.

LOW RATIO WEAPONS [DEPLOY > DAMAGE or damage slow]:

  Sincere Delusion Framing
    Deploy: HIGH [requires sustained narrative maintenance and
      institutional credibility]
    Damage: MED [CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING degraded; axis genuine
      but ungrounded; produces effort without result]
    Counter: LOW-MED [ground axis in actual outputs; Track C
      sufficient]
    Ratio: MED/HIGH = inefficient.

  Love Bombing then Withdrawal
    Deploy: HIGH [requires sustained relational investment]
    Damage: HIGH [SUBSTRATE REGENERATION targeted; relational
      substrate constructed then destroyed]
    Counter: LOW-MED [pattern recognition; name the cycle]
    Ratio: HIGH/HIGH = neutral.

  Isolation Construction
    Deploy: HIGH [requires controlling target's relational
      access; institutional or social leverage needed]
    Damage: HIGH [T1.4 destroyed; GENERATIVE CYCLE loses
      substrate source; C1 starved trajectory]
    Counter: MED [alternative relational substrate]
    Ratio: HIGH/HIGH = neutral.

SPECIAL CATEGORY -- SELF-DEPLOYING [no deployer required]:

  Fertile Lock [C3 failure, not weapon -- but weapon-equivalent]
    Deploy: ZERO [emerges from C3 failure; no actor required]
    Damage: HIGH [successors receive locked state; generational
      propagation at zero cost]
    Counter: EXTREME [C1 locked recovery has no primary confirmed
      case; requires cross-generational transmission method
      correction]
    Note: not a weapon in strict sense [no deployer intent] but
      weapon-equivalent in effect. Spontaneous extraction.

  Idle Cycle Deprivation [also self-deploying]
    Once SR deficit produces anxiety and anxiety produces
    input-seeking, weapon sustains without external input.

STRATEGIC IMPLICATIONS:

  Cheapest effective stack [C1 maximum pressure, low cost]:
    ICD + Rhythm Disruption + Intermittent Reinforcement.
    All three LOW-MED deploy cost. Combined: C1 under maximum
    sustained pressure with SR unable to recover.

  Hardest weapons to counter [external instrument required]:
    MSC, Calibration Strip, Formation Capture [early window].

  Fastest damage weapons [immediate effect]:
    Amnesia Manufacture [C2 destroyed directly], ECD [causal
    chain severed immediately], Boundary Manipulation.

  Most durable weapons [persist after deployment ends]:
    Formation Capture, Calibration Strip, Fertile Lock.

  Counter-cost rule: weapons targeting C2 first produce the
    hardest counters because the instrument needed to verify
    counter-effectiveness is the same instrument the weapon
    captured.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Every weapon in the index has independent psychological research
confirmation. The framework was built from fictional cases; the
psychology was built from real populations using different methods.
Independent convergence across two evidence types confirms structural
patterns are real not fictional artifacts.

Key researchers: Milgram [authority], Bandura [moral disengagement],
  Cialdini [influence], Kahneman/Tversky [cognitive bias], Lifton
  [thought reform], Herman [trauma], Maslach [burnout], Seligman
  [learned helplessness], Tajfel/Turner [social identity], Loftus
  [memory], Haidt [moral psychology], Bowlby [attachment],
  Mullainathan/Shafir [scarcity], Singer [cult psychology].

No weapon in the index lacks psychological confirmation.

==============================================================================

CASES [part of Section Six permanent reference]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Proof for: [what concept this demonstrates]
  Description
  Three-axis [where applicable]
  Weapons active [with quality]
  S: [case strength rating -- see CASE STRENGTH RATING in Section One]

Primary = measurement reference. Used when running readings.
Secondary = only added when variance from primary is substantively
informative. Not different enough = not added.

S-RATING KEY:
  S1 = ILLUSTRATION [fictional, no real-world anchor]
  S2 = WEAK THEORY [fictional with framework-consistent support, OR
       real-world single domain without controlled comparison]
  S3 = CONFIRMED [real-world E3+ AND M3+ AND F2+]
  S4 = STRONG [real-world E4 AND M4 AND F3+]
  Fictional cases cap at S2 regardless of library depth.

PRIMARY CASES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

[CASE:HELL_TAXONOMY]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENERATIVE CYCLE]
Hell taxonomy [Trench Crusade]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: Extractive Intrinsic type; compatible selfishness
    producing collective behavior without coordination. Mammon-type
    primary. Collective extraction without coordination requirement.
    Complexity ceiling preventing development beyond intensity scaling.
  Weapons active: none [structural].
  Cross-ref: [REF:HELL_TAXONOMY]

[CASE:BLOOD_SOAKED_COURT]
[PRIMARY_Q:SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY]
Blood-Soaked Court [Trench Crusade]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: Axis Substitution [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY]; High coherence
    + High capture simultaneously; Axis-Lock with genuine internal
    experience of captured axis as own values. Scarcity behavior
    negative -- axis stability from external infernal supply chain
    not internal. Three-axis: right, down, back.
  Weapons active: Axis Substitution [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY],
    Legitimacy Transfer [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY].
  Cross-ref: [REF:BLOOD_SOAKED_COURT] -- contrast [REF:CHURCH_MILITANT].

[CASE:CHURCH_MILITANT]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Church Militant [Trench Crusade]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: High coherence under sustained scarcity; same-setting
    contrast with [REF:BLOOD_SOAKED_COURT]. Scarcity behavior
    positive. Most useful same-setting contrast pair in library for
    demonstrating capture axis independence.
    Three-axis: right, up, front-mid.
  Weapons active [defensive]: Obligation Manufacture [MEASUREMENT
    INTEGRITY], Correction Theatre [CORRECTION CAPACITY].
  Cross-ref: [REF:CHURCH_MILITANT]

[CASE:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]
[PRIMARY_Q:SURPLUS PRODUCTION]
Imperium of Man [Warhammer 40K]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: Consumption at civilizational scale; substrate
    consumption invisible from inside own measurement surface.
    Net transfer: tithe, conscription, psyker collection extracting
    at accelerating rate while generative capacity declines across
    ten-thousand-year window. SUBSTRATE REGENERATION/SURPLUS
    DISTRIBUTION/CORRECTION TARGETING/OUTPUT INTEGRATION/SIGNAL
    READING/CAUSAL MEMORY all failing simultaneously -- most
    comprehensive extraction case in library.
    Three-axis: right of center, strongly down, mid.
  Weapons active: Normalcy Anchor [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY],
    Horizon Extension [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY], Sacrifice Frame
    [SURPLUS PRODUCTION], Legitimacy Transfer [TRANSMISSION
    SELECTIVITY].
  POLITICAL READING SPLIT [v15]:
    Right reads Imperium as legitimate by survival: 10,000 years
    under total external pressure = smart extraction with floor.
    Left reads as dystopia: suffering exists therefore system
    producing it is primary obstacle. TENSION DISSOLUTION failure
    -- utopian ending requires eliminating the tension that made
    the system real [False Coherence by design].
    Framework: Imperium scores low on almost every quality but
    survives because SR maintained and GC not fully collapsed.
    Smart extraction under total external pressure -- stupid
    extraction would have finished it centuries ago.
  Cross-ref: [REF:IMPERIUM_OF_MAN]

[CASE:WORD_BEARERS]
[PRIMARY_Q:TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]
Word Bearers [Warhammer 40K]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Proof for: Change-axis Lock; Fertile Lock institutional;
    Transmission Capture [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY];
    self-sustaining configuration. Pre-Monarchia: genuine High
    coherence. Monarchia censure: capture entry. Post-Monarchia:
    examination capacity running at full power justifying fixed
    conclusion. Isstvan III: purging correctable not disloyal.
    Most stable Chaos Legion because configuration is
    self-sustaining. Three-axis: far right, strongly down, back.
  Weapons active: Enantiodromia [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY], Transmission
    Capture [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY], Calibration Strip
    [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY].
  Cross-ref: [REF:WORD_BEARERS]

[CASE:ERGO_PROXY]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING]
Ergo Proxy [anime -- Manglobe, 2006]
  Type: Primary [S:1]
  Arc: CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING concealed from actors operating
    inside constructed identities. False Coherence city-state
    with MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY capture at population scale.
  ROMDO [False Coherence architecture]:
    High local coherence -- clean, ordered, functional city.
    Total substrate extraction from outside the dome. Population's
    MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY managed by AutoReivs. Citizens cannot
    read the full boundary cost.
  PROXY SYSTEM [CC monopolized then abandoned]:
    Proxies created to manage reconstruction after ecological
    collapse. CC monopolized at theological scale. Creators
    abandoned project off-world. Proxies left with purpose but
    no correction architecture above them. Axis drifts toward
    self-preservation.
  RE-L / VINCENT [CAG concealment as arc]:
    Both operating under constructed identities hiding actual
    axis from themselves. Entire narrative is T3.2 restoration
